```ts
import { computed, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { environment } from '@env/environment';
import { HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { patchState, signalStore, withComputed, withMethods, withState } from '@ngrx/signals';
import { rxMethod } from '@ngrx/signals/rxjs-interop';
import { tapResponse } from '@ngrx/operators';
import { EMPTY, catchError, firstValueFrom, concatMap, delay, exhaustMap, forkJoin, from, map, of, pipe, switchMap, take, takeWhile, tap, timer } from 'rxjs';
import { CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload, IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { IrAttachment, IrAttachmentSlot, IrPendingAttachment } from '@models/wwell-planning/ir-attachment.model';
import { WWELL_DELETE_DELAY_MS, WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS } from '@models/wwell-planning/wwell-planning-loading.constants';
import { SapAttachmentFile } from '@models/sap/sap-trigger.model';
import { SapService } from '@services/sap/sap.service';
import { S3Service } from '@services/s3/s3.service';
import { NotificationService } from '@shared/components/notification/notification.service';
import { AuthStore } from 'src/app/features/auth/store/auth.store';
import { mapSapWorkflowStatusToApprovalChain } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-approval.mapper';
import { mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-detail-response.mapper';
import { buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-draft.util';
import { WwellIrBulkSavePayload } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.model';
import { WwellIrLetterPdfService } from '../wwell-ir-letter-pdf.service';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-ir.service';
import { labelForSlot, slotFromObjectKey } from '../wwell-ir-attachments.config';
import {
  buildSapWorkflowTitle,
  WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID,
  WWELL_IR_SAP_RECALL_POLL_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
  WWELL_IR_SAP_RECALL_POLL_MS,
  WWELL_IR_SAP_SCENARIO_ID,
  WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS,
} from '../wwell-ir-sap.config';
import { isSapWorkflowRecalled } from '../wwell-ir.config';
import { WwellIrStoreState, initialWwellIrState } from './wwell-ir.state';

/** Raw base64 (no `data:` prefix) of a blob — the shape SAP's `attachment_files[].b64` expects. */
function blobToBase64(blob: Blob): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result).split(',')[1] ?? '');
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });
}

/** Prefers the backend's own `{ message }` (see ApiResponse<T> — every exad_agwa endpoint here
 *  returns that envelope on both success and error) over Angular's generic "Http failure
 *  response for ..." text, so a specific backend validation error (e.g. an Oracle constraint
 *  message) reaches the user instead of a vague fallback. */
function errorMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (err instanceof HttpErrorResponse) {
    // status 0 means the request never reached a server at all (refused connection, DNS failure,
    // CORS rejection, offline). There is no ApiResponse envelope to prefer, and `err.error` holds
    // the transport's own TypeError — surfacing its "Failed to fetch" as though it were a backend
    // message tells the user nothing, so the caller's own copy wins here.
    if (err.status === 0) return fallback;
    const body: unknown = err.error;
    const backendMessage = body && typeof body === 'object' ? (body as { message?: unknown }).message : undefined;
    if (typeof backendMessage === 'string' && backendMessage) return backendMessage;
    return err.message || fallback;
  }
  return err instanceof Error ? err.message : fallback;
}

/** Like errorMessage above, but prefers the SAP trigger endpoint's own `{ detail: [{ msg }] }`
 *  422 validation shape (SapValidationError) over the generic `{ message }` envelope every other
 *  exad_agwa endpoint here uses — so a real "field X is required" from SAP reaches the user
 *  instead of a vague fallback. */
function sapErrorMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (err instanceof HttpErrorResponse) {
    const body: unknown = err.error;
    const detail = body && typeof body === 'object' ? (body as { detail?: unknown }).detail : undefined;
    if (Array.isArray(detail)) {
      const messages = detail
        .map(item => (item && typeof item === 'object' ? (item as { msg?: unknown }).msg : undefined))
        .filter((msg): msg is string => typeof msg === 'string' && msg.length > 0);
      if (messages.length > 0) return messages.join('; ');
    }
  }
  return errorMessage(err, fallback);
}

/** Stamps the just-persisted SAP workflow onto the record: `sapWfId`/`sapWfStatusText` mirror
 *  exactly what `update_ir_sap_wf_prc` just wrote to sap_wf_id/sap_wf_status
 *  (WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS — submitting never asks SAP's own GET /display, so this is
 *  a local placeholder, not SAP's real text). `chain` is deliberately left untouched: nothing was
 *  fetched to refresh it with here — the next time this record is *viewed*
 *  (WwellIrStore.loadIrApprovalTimeline, via loadIrDetail or refreshIrApprovalStatus) is what
 *  reads SAP's real current status and chain back over this placeholder, everywhere it's shown
 *  (list badge, detail header badge, Approval Workflow sidebar) at once. */
function markSubmittedForReview(list: readonly IntegratedRequirement[], id: string, sapWfId: number, sapWfStatusText: string): IntegratedRequirement[] {
  // `chain: []` on purpose: a resubmission (after a Rejected/Cancelled/Recalled workflow) mints a brand
  // new SAP workflow, so the previous workflow's steps must not linger on the record — the letter and
  // the sidebar would otherwise keep showing the old table until SAP's own chain is read back.
  return list.map(item => (item.id === id ? { ...item, status: 'submit-for-review' as const, sapWfId, sapWfStatusText, chain: [] } : item));
}

function upsertByIrSeq(list: readonly IntegratedRequirement[], created: IntegratedRequirement): IntegratedRequirement[] {
  const existingIndex = created.irSeq != null ? list.findIndex(item => item.irSeq === created.irSeq) : -1;
  if (existingIndex === -1) return [created, ...list];
  // A brand-new record's `created` (see createIr's own buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload
  // fallback) has no way to know the record's real approval chain (that's a separate fetch; see
  // loadIrApprovalTimeline), so it always starts a fresh "just initiated" chain. Harmless for a
  // first-time INSERT (nothing to preserve yet), but would otherwise wipe out an already-in-
  // progress/approved record's real chain on every subsequent save. Keep whichever chain this
  // record already had.
  return list.map((item, index) => (index === existingIndex ? { ...created, chain: item.chain } : item));
}

export const WwellIrStore = signalStore(
  withState<WwellIrStoreState>(initialWwellIrState),

  withComputed(
    ({
      irsLoading,
      linkedRequestWells,
      linkedRequestWellsLoading,
      gwdOpsMembers,
      aquifers,
      stratInfos,
      irsError,
      linkedRequestWellsError,
      gwdOpsMembersError,
      createIrError,
      sapTriggerError,
      sapRecallError,
      irDetailError,
      irApprovalTimelineError,
      deleteIrError,
      attachmentError,
    }) => ({
      isLoading: computed(() => irsLoading() || linkedRequestWellsLoading()),
      /** Trimmed {value,label} view of linkedRequestWells for the "Link to Request" picker itself —
       *  see wwell-ir-editor.component.ts's `applyLinkedRequestWell` for where the full record
       *  (looked up from linkedRequestWells by this same value) drives the auto-populate. */
      linkedRequestWellOptions: computed(() => linkedRequestWells().map(well => ({ value: String(well.prewapSeq), label: well.wellName }))),
      /** FULL_NAME option list for the Geologist Contact "Primary"/"Secondary" pickers — value and
       *  label are the same string; the real EMPNUM to persist is resolved separately via
       *  `geologistEmpNumByName` below at save time. */
      /** {value: code, label: "CODE - description"} for every Formation/Aquifer dropdown — the code is
       *  what gets saved; the description (absent in the JSON fallback list) is display only. */
      aquiferOptions: computed(() =>
        aquifers().map(aquifer => ({ value: aquifer.code, label: aquifer.description ? `${aquifer.code} - ${aquifer.description}` : aquifer.code })),
      ),
      /** {value, label} = st_long_cd for the Formation Tops "Formation" dropdown — the name is both what
       *  the user sees and what gets saved, so there is no separate code to resolve at save time. */
      formationOptions: computed(() => stratInfos().map(strat => ({ value: strat.description, label: strat.description }))),
      geologistOptions: computed(() => gwdOpsMembers().map(member => member.fullName)),
      /** FULL_NAME → EMPNUM, threaded into buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload's WwellIrSaveContext so
       *  cont_geologist/sub_cont_geologist persist the real employee number, not a display name. */
      geologistEmpNumByName: computed<Readonly<Record<string, number>>>(() =>
        Object.fromEntries(gwdOpsMembers().map(member => [member.fullName, member.empNum])),
      ),
      /** Inverse of geologistEmpNumByName - EMPNUM → FULL_NAME, so a detail/list fetch that comes
       *  back with exad_gwd_ir_header.cont_geologist/sub_cont_geologist (a raw EMPNUM) can be
       *  redisplayed as the name the Geologist Contact picker showed when it was saved. Without
       *  this, mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement has no way to turn that EMPNUM back
       *  into a name and the field renders blank even though the correct value is in the DB. */
      geologistNameByEmpNum: computed<Readonly<Record<number, string>>>(() =>
        Object.fromEntries(gwdOpsMembers().map(member => [member.empNum, member.fullName])),
      ),
      activeErrors: computed(() =>
        [
          irsError(),
          linkedRequestWellsError(),
          gwdOpsMembersError(),
          createIrError(),
          sapTriggerError(),
          sapRecallError(),
          irDetailError(),
          irApprovalTimelineError(),
          deleteIrError(),
          attachmentError(),
        ].filter((message): message is string => message !== null),
      ),
      hasError: computed(
        () =>
          irsError() !== null ||
          linkedRequestWellsError() !== null ||
          gwdOpsMembersError() !== null ||
          createIrError() !== null ||
          sapTriggerError() !== null ||
          sapRecallError() !== null ||
          irDetailError() !== null ||
          irApprovalTimelineError() !== null ||
          deleteIrError() !== null ||
          attachmentError() !== null,
      ),
    }),
  ),

  withMethods((
    store,
    svc = inject(WwellIrService),
    sap = inject(SapService),
    s3 = inject(S3Service),
    letterPdf = inject(WwellIrLetterPdfService),
    authStore = inject(AuthStore),
    notify = inject(NotificationService),
  ) => {
    // Step 3, reached only after SAP has accepted the workflow (step 2): stamps the id and a
    // local "In Progress" placeholder status onto the IR header (update_ir_sap_wf_prc — `seq`
    // lands in sap_wf_id, WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS in sap_wf_status). Deliberately does
    // NOT ask SAP's own GET /display for the real status first — see that constant's own doc
    // comment for why a just-created workflow can safely assume "in progress" without asking, and
    // WwellIrStore.loadIrApprovalTimeline for where the real status/chain get read back instead
    // (any time the record is viewed, or refreshIrApprovalStatus is called on demand).
    //
    // Deliberately not called on a failed trigger: sap_wf_id/sap_wf_status describe a workflow
    // that exists in SAP, so recording one for a push SAP never accepted would leave the header
    // pointing at an id no SAP workflow was ever created under.
    function persistSapWorkflow(ir: IntegratedRequirement, irSeq: number, seq: number): void {
      svc.updateIrSapWorkflow(irSeq, seq, WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS).subscribe({
        next: () =>
          patchState(store, {
            updatingIrStatus: false,
            sapTriggerError: null,
            irs: markSubmittedForReview(store.irs(), ir.id, seq, WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS),
          }),
        error: (err: unknown) =>
          patchState(store, {
            updatingIrStatus: false,
            sapTriggerError: errorMessage(err, 'SAP workflow triggered, but failed to record it on the integrated requirement'),
          }),
      });
    }

    // Pulls every stored attachment's bytes back from the S3 proxy as base64 for the trigger's
    // `attachment_files`, preceded by the complete IR letter — the "Download PDF"
    // output minus the Approval List table (omitted for SAP only; wellbore diagram captured from an
    // off-screen Letter render, attachments appended as trailing pages; see WwellIrLetterPdfService). All-or-nothing: if any read fails the submit
    // fails, rather than silently sending SAP a review package with documents missing.
    function loadSapAttachmentFiles(ir: IntegratedRequirement, newWorkflowSeq: number) {
      // Always the store's current record for the attachments (an upload/replace since the editor
      // handed `ir` over must not be missed), and always the NEW workflow for the letter: `ir` still
      // carries the previous workflow's id, status text (e.g. Rejected → a red stamp) and approval
      // chain on a resubmission, which is exactly what the letter's workflow table would print.
      const current = store.irs().find(item => item.id === ir.id) ?? ir;
      const submission: IntegratedRequirement = {
        ...ir,
        attachments: current.attachments,
        sapWfId: newWorkflowSeq,
        sapWfStatusText: WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS,
        chain: [],
      };
      const letter$ = from(letterPdf.buildLetterPdf(submission, true)).pipe(
        switchMap(blob => blobToBase64(blob)),
        map((b64): SapAttachmentFile => ({ file_name: `${ir.code}-letter.pdf`, b64 })),
      );
      const stored$ = submission.attachments.map(attachment =>
        s3.fetch(attachment.objectKey).pipe(map((response): SapAttachmentFile => ({ file_name: attachment.fileName, b64: response.content }))),
      );
      return forkJoin([letter$, ...stored$]);
    }

    // Steps 1-2: mint a workflow id (get_wf_next_id_fnc), then push the workflow to SAP under it.
    // workflow_id sent to SAP is the raw minted seq, as-is — no network id or scenario suffix.
    function triggerSapWorkflow(ir: IntegratedRequirement, irSeq: number): void {
      const networkId = authStore.networkId() ?? '';
      svc.getNextSapWorkflowSeq().subscribe({
        next: seq => {
          loadSapAttachmentFiles(ir, seq)
            .pipe(
              switchMap(attachmentFiles =>
                sap.trigger({
                  workflow_title: buildSapWorkflowTitle(ir.code, ir.requestReference.wellName, environment.production),
                  initiator_network_id: networkId,
                  requester_network_id: networkId,
                  process_id: WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID,
                  scenario_id: WWELL_IR_SAP_SCENARIO_ID,
                  workflow_id: String(seq),
                  attachment_files: attachmentFiles,
                  body: [],
                }),
              ),
            )
            .subscribe({
              next: () => persistSapWorkflow(ir, irSeq, seq),
              // SAP refused the workflow (or an attachment couldn't be read), so there is nothing to
              // stamp — surface the failure and leave the header exactly as it was.
              error: (err: unknown) =>
                patchState(store, {
                  updatingIrStatus: false,
                  sapTriggerError: sapErrorMessage(err, 'Failed to trigger the SAP review workflow'),
                }),
            });
        },
        // Nothing to trigger or persist without a real id — never call SAP with a fabricated one.
        error: (err: unknown) =>
          patchState(store, { updatingIrStatus: false, sapTriggerError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to generate a SAP workflow id') }),
      });
    }

    // Two calls against SAP AAE: DELETE /recall against the workflow's existing id (no new id to
    // mint — recall acts on the workflow submitIrForReview already created), then GET /display for
    // the real, current status/chain. The real recall response is a bare acknowledgement
    // (`{ message: string }`), not a workflow-status payload — confirmed live in production, where
    // treating it as one crashed reading `.header.wf_status_text` off a response with no `header` at
    // all. There's nothing to read a status out of the recall response itself, so ask SAP directly
    // afterward, the same way loadIrApprovalTimeline does.
    //
    // Nothing needs persisting back to the IR header here either: recall doesn't mint a new
    // sapWfId (the header's existing one is still correct), and every place that shows
    // sapWfStatusText already gets it fresh from this same live GET on every selection/reload
    // (loadIrDetail → loadIrApprovalTimeline) — a value guessed from the recall response and PATCHed
    // onto the header would only be a redundant placeholder, immediately overwritten.
    //
    // `recallingIr` deliberately stays true across both calls, not just the first — it's what the
    // editor's footer button reads for its spinner, and flipping it false the moment the recall ack
    // lands would stop the spinner mid-action, right as the follow-up status fetch starts.
    function recallSapWorkflow(irSeq: number, sapWfId: number): void {
      const networkId = authStore.networkId() ?? '';
      sap.recall(WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID, sapWfId, networkId).subscribe({
        // SAP applies a recall asynchronously (~5-7s), so one immediate GET /display still reads the
        // old status. Poll until it reports Recalled (each read is shown as it arrives), capped at
        // WWELL_IR_SAP_RECALL_POLL_MAX_ATTEMPTS; a failed read is just retried on the next tick.
        next: () => {
          let sawStatus = false;
          timer(WWELL_IR_SAP_RECALL_POLL_MS, WWELL_IR_SAP_RECALL_POLL_MS)
            .pipe(
              take(WWELL_IR_SAP_RECALL_POLL_MAX_ATTEMPTS),
              concatMap(() => sap.getWorkflowStatus(WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID, sapWfId).pipe(catchError(() => of(null)))),
              tap(status => {
                if (!status) return;
                sawStatus = true;
                patchState(store, {
                  irs: store.irs().map(item =>
                    item.irSeq === irSeq
                      ? { ...item, chain: mapSapWorkflowStatusToApprovalChain(status), sapWfStatusText: status.header.wf_status_text }
                      : item,
                  ),
                });
              }),
              takeWhile(status => !status || !isSapWorkflowRecalled(status.header.wf_status_text), true),
            )
            .subscribe({
              complete: () =>
                patchState(store, {
                  recallingIr: false,
                  sapRecallError: sawStatus ? null : 'Workflow recalled, but failed to refresh its latest status',
                }),
            });
        },
        // SAP refused the recall — surface the failure and leave the header exactly as it was, same
        // as a refused trigger.
        error: (err: unknown) =>
          patchState(store, {
            recallingIr: false,
            sapRecallError: sapErrorMessage(err, 'Failed to recall the SAP review workflow'),
          }),
      });
    }

    const loadIrs = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { irsLoading: true, irsError: null })),
        switchMap(() =>
          svc.getIrs().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: irs => patchState(store, { irs, irsLoading: false }),
              error: (err: unknown) => patchState(store, { irsLoading: false, irsError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load integrated requirements') }),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    // exhaustMap, not switchMap: this fires once at list init and again on the first selection —
    // milliseconds apart whenever the list is already in the store — and switchMap aborted the first
    // request (a red "(canceled)" row in the network tab) in favor of an identical second one.
    const loadLinkedRequestWells = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => {
          console.log('[IR:link] loadLinkedRequestWells — fetching GET /wells/eligible-for-ir');
          patchState(store, { linkedRequestWellsLoading: true, linkedRequestWellsError: null });
        }),
        exhaustMap(() =>
          svc.getEligibleWellsForIr().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: linkedRequestWells => {
                console.log('[IR:link] loadLinkedRequestWells — response received', { count: linkedRequestWells.length, linkedRequestWells });
                patchState(store, { linkedRequestWells, linkedRequestWellsLoading: false });
              },
              error: (err: unknown) => {
                console.log('[IR:link] loadLinkedRequestWells — request failed', err);
                patchState(store, {
                  linkedRequestWellsLoading: false,
                  linkedRequestWellsError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load wells eligible for IR initiation'),
                });
              },
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    // Stable reference data (not tied to a specific IR selection, unlike loadLinkedRequestWells) —
    // loaded once at list-page init, see WwellIrComponent.ngOnInit.
    const loadGwdOpsMembers = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { gwdOpsMembersLoading: true, gwdOpsMembersError: null })),
        switchMap(() =>
          svc.getGwdOpsMembers().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: gwdOpsMembers => patchState(store, { gwdOpsMembers, gwdOpsMembersLoading: false }),
              error: (err: unknown) =>
                patchState(store, { gwdOpsMembersLoading: false, gwdOpsMembersError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load GWD ops members') }),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    const loadStratInfos = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        switchMap(() =>
          svc.getStratInfo().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: stratInfos => patchState(store, { stratInfos }),
              error: (err: unknown) => notify.error(errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load formation list')),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    const loadAquifers = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        switchMap(() =>
          svc.getAquifers().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: aquifers => patchState(store, { aquifers }),
              error: (err: unknown) => notify.error(errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load aquifer list')),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    const loadDropdownMenus = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        switchMap(() =>
          svc.getDropdownMenus().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: dropdownMenus => patchState(store, { dropdownMenus }),
              // Non-fatal: the dropdowns just stay empty (no arrow) until a reload succeeds.
              error: () => undefined,
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    // Keyed by ir_seq (the real primary key once an IR is saved) — switchMap cancels a still-in-
    // flight fetch for a previously-selected row if the user picks another one before it resolves,
    // so a slow first response can never land after (and clobber) a faster later selection.
    const loadIrDetail = rxMethod<number>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { irDetailLoading: true, irDetailError: null })),
        switchMap(irSeq =>
          svc.getIrDetailBlocks(irSeq).pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: blocks => {
                const existing = store.irs().find(item => item.irSeq === irSeq);
                // Nothing to merge onto if the summary row isn't loaded (shouldn't happen from the
                // normal select-a-row flow) — just clear the loading flag and leave state as-is.
                if (!existing) {
                  patchState(store, { irDetailLoading: false });
                  return;
                }
                const merged = mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement(existing, blocks, store.geologistNameByEmpNum());
                patchState(store, { irs: upsertByIrSeq(store.irs(), merged), irDetailLoading: false });
                // Only the detail fetch's exad_gwd_ir_header block carries sap_wf_id (the list
                // endpoint doesn't — see wwell-ir-summary.mapper.ts), so this is the first point a
                // real SAP lookup becomes possible for a freshly-selected record.
                loadIrApprovalTimeline({ irSeq, sapWfId: merged.sapWfId });
              },
              error: (err: unknown) =>
                patchState(store, { irDetailLoading: false, irDetailError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load integrated requirement detail') }),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    // Feeds the Approval Workflow sidebar straight from SAP's own GET /display, looked up by the
    // IR's sapWfId (== wf_obj_id, the raw id minted by Submit for Review — see
    // wwell-ir-sap.config.ts). An IR never submitted has no sapWfId and therefore no SAP workflow
    // to look up — that case clears the chain locally instead of calling SAP with nothing to ask
    // about. Same switchMap-cancels-a-stale-fetch reasoning as loadIrDetail applies when the
    // selection changes quickly.
    const loadIrApprovalTimeline = rxMethod<{ irSeq: number; sapWfId: number | null }>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { irApprovalTimelineLoading: true, irApprovalTimelineError: null })),
        switchMap(({ irSeq, sapWfId }) => {
          // Nothing to look up yet — leave `chain` exactly as it was (the initial local preview;
          // see buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload) rather than blank it, since chainBorderColor
          // reads it for the detail panel's accent strip in both view and edit mode, not only the
          // view-mode sidebar this gates (wwell-ir.component.html).
          if (sapWfId == null) {
            patchState(store, { irApprovalTimelineLoading: false });
            return EMPTY;
          }
          return sap.getWorkflowStatus(WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID, sapWfId).pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: status => {
                patchState(store, {
                  // header.wf_status_text is the real workflow status SAP is showing right now —
                  // surfaced on the list badge (wwell-ir.component.html) in place of the local
                  // "Submit for Review" stand-in markSubmittedForReview sets optimistically, so a
                  // freshly-submitted record doesn't keep showing that stale label once SAP's own
                  // status is known.
                  irs: store.irs().map(item =>
                    item.irSeq === irSeq
                      ? { ...item, chain: mapSapWorkflowStatusToApprovalChain(status), sapWfStatusText: status.header.wf_status_text }
                      : item,
                  ),
                  irApprovalTimelineLoading: false,
                });
              },
              error: (err: unknown) =>
                patchState(store, {
                  irApprovalTimelineLoading: false,
                  irApprovalTimelineError: sapErrorMessage(err, 'Failed to load the SAP approval workflow status'),
                }),
            }),
          );
        }),
      ),
    );

    /** Every object stored under this IR's prefix (trailing slash so IR 900's query can never match
     *  IR 9006's objects — see S3Service.search), newest-first from the proxy and reversed here to
     *  oldest-uploaded-first (matches `irAttachmentsFor`'s doc comment). A key that doesn't parse as
     *  one of this app's own slots (see `slotFromObjectKey`) is silently skipped rather than shown
     *  with a broken slot. */
    const loadIrAttachments = rxMethod<number>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { attachmentsLoading: true, attachmentError: null })),
        switchMap(irSeq =>
          s3.search(`ir-attachments/ir-${irSeq}/`).pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: ({ objects }) => {
                const attachments: IrAttachment[] = [...objects]
                  .reverse()
                  .flatMap(object => {
                    const slot = slotFromObjectKey(object.OBJ_KEY);
                    if (!slot) return [];
                    return [
                      {
                        slot,
                        objectKey: object.OBJ_KEY,
                        label: labelForSlot(slot),
                        fileName: object.ORIGINAL_FILENAME,
                        sizeBytes: object.OBJ_SIZE,
                        contentType: object.CONTENT_TYPE_CD,
                      },
                    ];
                  });
                patchState(store, {
                  irs: store.irs().map(item => (item.irSeq === irSeq ? { ...item, attachments } : item)),
                  attachmentsLoading: false,
                });
              },
              error: (err: unknown) =>
                patchState(store, { attachmentsLoading: false, attachmentError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load attachments') }),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    /** Appends a newly-uploaded attachment onto the record, leaving every existing one (in this
     *  slot or any other) untouched — a slot accumulates attachments rather than holding just one. */
    const appendAttachment = (irSeq: number, attachment: IrAttachment) =>
      store.irs().map(item => (item.irSeq === irSeq ? { ...item, attachments: [...item.attachments, attachment] } : item));

    /** Drops one specific stored attachment (matched by its object key, its only identity) off the
     *  record, leaving every other attachment in place. */
    const withoutAttachment = (irSeq: number, objectKey: string) =>
      store.irs().map(item =>
        item.irSeq === irSeq ? { ...item, attachments: item.attachments.filter(existing => existing.objectKey !== objectKey) } : item,
      );

    /** Marks one pending file as mid-upload (or clears that marking) — shared by the single-item
     *  and sweep upload paths below so both track busy/progress identically per file id. */
    function setPendingUploadBusy(id: string, percent: number | null): void {
      patchState(store, {
        uploadingPendingIds: new Set(store.uploadingPendingIds()).add(id),
        uploadProgressByPendingId: { ...store.uploadProgressByPendingId(), [id]: percent },
      });
    }

    function clearPendingUploadBusy(id: string): void {
      const uploadingPendingIds = new Set(store.uploadingPendingIds());
      uploadingPendingIds.delete(id);
      const uploadProgressByPendingId = { ...store.uploadProgressByPendingId() };
      delete uploadProgressByPendingId[id];
      patchState(store, { uploadingPendingIds, uploadProgressByPendingId });
    }

    /** Drops one staged file (by id) from its slot's pending list, leaving any others staged there untouched. */
    function withoutPendingAttachment(slot: IrAttachmentSlot, id: string): Partial<Record<IrAttachmentSlot, readonly IrPendingAttachment[]>> {
      const remaining = (store.pendingAttachments()[slot] ?? []).filter(item => item.id !== id);
      return { ...store.pendingAttachments(), [slot]: remaining };
    }

    /**
     * Uploads one staged file straight to the S3 object-store proxy — the sole upload call now,
     * with no agwa-planning involvement at all (see ir-attachment.model.ts's class doc comment).
     * Independent of every other file in flight — each call tracks its own busy/progress by the
     * pending item's id (see `uploadingPendingIds`/`uploadProgressByPendingId`), so clicking several
     * rows' Upload buttons in a row fires that many concurrent requests rather than queuing behind
     * one another. Clears the staging only after the proxy confirms, so a failed upload leaves the
     * file in its slot to retry rather than silently dropping the user's pick.
     */
    function uploadOne(irSeq: number, slot: IrAttachmentSlot, item: IrPendingAttachment): void {
      setPendingUploadBusy(item.id, null);
      patchState(store, { attachmentError: null });
      const networkId = authStore.networkId() ?? '';
      const prefix = `ir-attachments/ir-${irSeq}/${slot}`;
      s3.store(item.file, prefix, networkId, item.file.name).subscribe({
        next: ({ percent, response }) => {
          if (!response) {
            setPendingUploadBusy(item.id, percent);
            return;
          }
          const attachment: IrAttachment = {
            slot,
            objectKey: response.key,
            label: labelForSlot(slot),
            fileName: item.file.name,
            sizeBytes: item.file.size,
            contentType: response.content_type,
          };
          clearPendingUploadBusy(item.id);
          patchState(store, {
            irs: appendAttachment(irSeq, attachment),
            pendingAttachments: withoutPendingAttachment(slot, item.id),
          });
          notify.info(`${attachment.label} uploaded.`);
        },
        error: (err: unknown) => {
          clearPendingUploadBusy(item.id);
          patchState(store, { attachmentError: errorMessage(err, `Failed to upload the ${slot} attachment`) });
        },
      });
    }

    return {
      loadIrs(): void {
        loadIrs();
      },

      loadIrDetail(irSeq: number): void {
        loadIrDetail(irSeq);
      },

      loadIrAttachments(irSeq: number): void {
        loadIrAttachments(irSeq);
      },

      /**
       * Stages a picked file alongside whatever else is already staged for that slot — attaching
       * never replaces, only adds. Attaching never uploads on its own either: the user sends it with
       * the file's own upload action (`uploadPendingAttachment`), or, for a New IR with no ir_seq to
       * upload against yet, the first save flushes everything staged via `uploadPendingAttachments`.
       */
      addPendingAttachment(slot: IrAttachmentSlot, file: File): string {
        const item: IrPendingAttachment = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), file };
        const existing = store.pendingAttachments()[slot] ?? [];
        patchState(store, {
          pendingAttachments: { ...store.pendingAttachments(), [slot]: [...existing, item] },
          attachmentError: null,
        });
        return item.id;
      },

      /** Sends one staged file. See `uploadOne` above for why this is safe to call for several
       *  files at once without them fighting over shared progress/busy state. */
      uploadPendingAttachment(irSeq: number, slot: IrAttachmentSlot, id: string): void {
        const item = (store.pendingAttachments()[slot] ?? []).find(candidate => candidate.id === id);
        if (!item) return;
        uploadOne(irSeq, slot, item);
      },

      /** Un-stages one file without sending it — the user changed their mind before uploading. */
      removePendingAttachment(slot: IrAttachmentSlot, id: string): void {
        patchState(store, { pendingAttachments: withoutPendingAttachment(slot, id) });
      },

      /** Drops every held file — the draft was abandoned, or a different record was selected. */
      clearPendingAttachments(): void {
        patchState(store, { pendingAttachments: {} });
      },

      /**
       * Uploads everything held for a draft that has just been assigned `irSeq` — every staged file
       * in every slot, each independently (see `uploadOne`). State is cleared up front so a second
       * save can't re-upload the same files, and a failure on one file doesn't abort the others:
       * each reports its own error and the rest continue.
       */
      uploadPendingAttachments(irSeq: number): void {
        const bySlot = store.pendingAttachments();
        patchState(store, { pendingAttachments: {} });
        (Object.entries(bySlot) as [IrAttachmentSlot, readonly IrPendingAttachment[]][]).forEach(([slot, items]) =>
          items.forEach(item => uploadOne(irSeq, slot, item)),
        );
      },

      /** Deletes one specific stored attachment by its object key — its only identity now. Tracked
       *  by that same key in `deletingAttachmentKeys` so only this one row shows busy, not the rest
       *  of the slot's list. Calls the S3 object-store proxy directly — there is no backend
       *  round-trip for attachments at all anymore (see ir-attachment.model.ts's class doc comment). */
      deleteIrAttachment(irSeq: number, objectKey: string): void {
        const networkId = authStore.networkId() ?? '';
        patchState(store, { deletingAttachmentKeys: new Set(store.deletingAttachmentKeys()).add(objectKey), attachmentError: null });
        s3.delete(objectKey, networkId).subscribe({
          next: () => {
            const deletingAttachmentKeys = new Set(store.deletingAttachmentKeys());
            deletingAttachmentKeys.delete(objectKey);
            patchState(store, { irs: withoutAttachment(irSeq, objectKey), deletingAttachmentKeys });
          },
          error: (err: unknown) => {
            const deletingAttachmentKeys = new Set(store.deletingAttachmentKeys());
            deletingAttachmentKeys.delete(objectKey);
            patchState(store, { deletingAttachmentKeys, attachmentError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to remove the attachment') });
          },
        });
      },

      loadLinkedRequestWells(): void {
        loadLinkedRequestWells();
      },

      loadGwdOpsMembers(): void {
        loadGwdOpsMembers();
      },

      loadAquifers(): void {
        loadAquifers();
      },

      loadStratInfos(): void {
        loadStratInfos();
      },

      loadDropdownMenus(): void {
        loadDropdownMenus();
      },

      /**
       * No optimistic echo: the backend only ever confirms an `ir_seq` (see WwellIrSaveResult),
       * so this waits out a real follow-up GET (getIrDetailBlocks) and merges *that* onto the
       * record's last known-real state before `irs` ever changes — never onto whatever was just
       * typed. A field with no backing column at all (client, targetAquifer, targetDepthFt... see
       * mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement's own doc comment) simply won't reflect the
       * edit once this settles, since there is nowhere real to read it back from — better an
       * honest revert than a value that only looks saved until the next reload.
       *
       * `creatingIr` stays true for both round-trips (POST, then GET), so the spinner is what
       * covers the extra latency — nothing renders in between.
       */
      createIr(payload: CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload, irBulkPayload: WwellIrBulkSavePayload): void {
        patchState(store, { creatingIr: true, createIrError: null });
        svc
          .createIr(irBulkPayload)
          .pipe(
            delay(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS),
            switchMap(irSeq => {
              if (irSeq === null) return of({ irSeq, blocks: null });
              return svc.getIrDetailBlocks(irSeq).pipe(
                map(blocks => ({ irSeq, blocks })),
                // The save itself already succeeded here — a hiccup on this second, read-only
                // call must not read as "the save failed" (see the `blocks === null` branch below).
                catchError(() => of({ irSeq, blocks: null })),
              );
            }),
          )
          .subscribe({
            next: ({ irSeq, blocks }) => {
              if (irSeq === null) {
                // No identity to file this under at all — shouldn't happen for a genuine backend
                // success, so there's nothing to merge or select; just stop spinning.
                patchState(store, { creatingIr: false });
                return;
              }
              if (blocks === null) {
                // Confirmed saved (a real ir_seq came back) but the follow-up detail fetch failed
                // — record the real identity so a resave correctly UPDATEs instead of re-INSERTing,
                // but leave `irs` untouched rather than show anything unconfirmed.
                patchState(store, {
                  draftIrSeq: irSeq,
                  creatingIr: false,
                  createIrError: 'Saved, but the latest data failed to reload — refresh to see it.',
                });
                return;
              }
              const existing = store.irs().find(item => item.irSeq === irSeq);
              const base =
                existing ?? buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload(payload, { id: `IR-${irSeq}`, code: `IR-${irSeq}`, irSeq });
              const merged = mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement(base, blocks, store.geologistNameByEmpNum());
              patchState(store, {
                irs: upsertByIrSeq(store.irs(), merged),
                draftIrSeq: irSeq,
                creatingIr: false,
              });
              // A brand-new record's local draft never had a real sapWfId — this GET's own
              // exad_gwd_ir_header block is the first point one could exist (a resave of an
              // already-submitted IR).
              loadIrApprovalTimeline({ irSeq, sapWfId: merged.sapWfId });
            },
            error: (err: unknown) => patchState(store, { creatingIr: false, createIrError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to create integrated requirement') }),
          });
      },

      /**
       * "Submit for Review" is the SAP workflow submission, in three steps against two backends:
       * mint a workflow id (agwa-planning's get_wf_next_id_fnc) → push the workflow to SAP AAE
       * under that id (SapService.trigger) → record the id and a local "in progress" placeholder
       * on the IR header (update_ir_sap_wf_prc). Deliberately does not also call SAP's own GET
       * /display to ask for a real status — see WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS's own doc
       * comment — so submitting stays fast and only ever costs one round-trip to SAP itself.
       *
       * There is no separate IR status-transition call: exad_gwd_ir_header has no status column of
       * its own, so the SAP workflow columns this writes ARE the record of having submitted.
       * `updatingIrStatus` stays true across all three so the editor's Submit button spins for the
       * whole action rather than flickering between steps.
       */
      submitIrForReview(ir: IntegratedRequirement): void {
        // A draft that was never saved has no ir_seq to file the workflow under, so there would be
        // nothing to record the outcome against — refuse before calling SAP rather than after.
        if (ir.irSeq == null) {
          patchState(store, { sapTriggerError: 'Save the integrated requirement before submitting it for review.' });
          return;
        }
        if (!ir.attachments.some(attachment => attachment.slot === 'location-map')) {
          patchState(store, { sapTriggerError: 'Attach a Location Map before submitting for review.' });
          return;
        }
        patchState(store, { updatingIrStatus: true, sapTriggerError: null });
        triggerSapWorkflow(ir, ir.irSeq);
      },

      /** "Recall" pulls back an already-submitted workflow — the editor only shows the button once
       *  `sapWfId` is set (see WwellIrEditorComponent.canRecallIr), but this guards it again since
       *  the store method is the one place that actually matters: there is no workflow to recall
       *  without a real `irSeq`/`sapWfId` pair to act on. */
      recallIr(ir: IntegratedRequirement): void {
        if (ir.irSeq == null || ir.sapWfId == null) {
          patchState(store, { sapRecallError: 'This integrated requirement has not been submitted for review.' });
          return;
        }
        patchState(store, { recallingIr: true, sapRecallError: null });
        recallSapWorkflow(ir.irSeq, ir.sapWfId);
      },

      /** On-demand counterpart to the automatic loadIrDetail → loadIrApprovalTimeline refresh: lets
       *  the UI (the Approval Workflow sidebar's refresh button, the detail header's status badge)
       *  pull SAP's real current status/chain for an already-selected record without needing a
       *  reselect. A no-op for a record with no `sapWfId` — there is no SAP workflow to ask about
       *  yet, same guard `loadIrApprovalTimeline` itself already applies. */
      refreshIrApprovalStatus(irSeq: number, sapWfId: number | null): void {
        if (sapWfId == null) return;
        loadIrApprovalTimeline({ irSeq, sapWfId });
      },

      /** Reads SAP's live status/chain for the record's CURRENT workflow, writes it into the store
       *  (so the screen matches) and returns the record with it applied — what the letter/PDF must
       *  print, rather than whatever was cached when the record was last opened. Resolves with the
       *  record unchanged when it has no `sapWfId` or SAP can't be reached: a SAP outage must not
       *  block a download, it just falls back to the last known workflow. */
      async syncApprovalStatus(ir: IntegratedRequirement): Promise<IntegratedRequirement> {
        if (ir.irSeq == null || ir.sapWfId == null) return ir;
        try {
          const status = await firstValueFrom(sap.getWorkflowStatus(WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID, ir.sapWfId));
          const fresh = { chain: mapSapWorkflowStatusToApprovalChain(status), sapWfStatusText: status.header.wf_status_text };
          patchState(store, { irs: store.irs().map(item => (item.irSeq === ir.irSeq ? { ...item, ...fresh } : item)) });
          return { ...ir, ...fresh };
        } catch {
          return ir;
        }
      },

      /** Deletes the whole IR (header row, cascading to casing/tops/hydrogeology/water — see
       *  WwellIrService.deleteIr) and drops it from `irs` on success. `irSeq` must already be a
       *  persisted record's — there's nothing to delete server-side for a local-only draft. */
      deleteIr(irSeq: number): void {
        patchState(store, { deletingIr: true, deleteIrError: null });
        svc
          .deleteIr(irSeq)
          .pipe(delay(WWELL_DELETE_DELAY_MS))
          .subscribe({
            next: () => patchState(store, { irs: store.irs().filter(item => item.irSeq !== irSeq), deletingIr: false }),
            error: (err: unknown) => patchState(store, { deletingIr: false, deleteIrError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to delete integrated requirement') }),
          });
      },
    };
  }),
);
```

## [A] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-coordinates.util.spec.ts

```ts
import { formatUtmCoordinates, toUtmAxes } from './wwell-ir-coordinates.util';

describe('wwell-ir coordinates', () => {
  it('composes northing/easting without a unit', () => {
    expect(formatUtmCoordinates(3043496.7774, 766571.9346, 37)).toBe('Northing 3043496.7774, Easting 766571.9346, Zone 37');
  });

  it('splits the composed string into bare values', () => {
    expect(toUtmAxes('Northing 3043496.7774, Easting 766571.9346, Zone 37').map(a => [a.label, a.value])).toEqual([
      ['Northing', '3043496.7774'],
      ['Easting', '766571.9346'],
      ['UTM Zone', '37'],
    ]);
  });

  it('still reads IRs saved with the old "m" suffix, and shows them without it', () => {
    expect(toUtmAxes('Northing 3043496.7774m, Easting 766571.9346 m, Zone 37').map(a => a.value)).toEqual([
      '3043496.7774',
      '766571.9346',
      '37',
    ]);
  });

  it('keeps free text whole', () => {
    expect(toUtmAxes('near the old pump station')).toEqual([{ label: 'Coordinates', value: 'near the old pump station', icon: 'location_on' }]);
  });
});
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-coordinates.util.ts

```ts
/** One piece of a well's surface location, ready to print: "Northing", "3043496.7774". No unit
 *  conversion happens anywhere in here — `exad_rcd_prewap.surfacen`/`surfacee` are real UTM
 *  northing/easting meters in production and `sv_utm_zn` is the zone they were measured in; this
 *  app shows all three exactly as the source gives them rather than computing lat/long from them
 *  (see docs/work.md's "coordinates are UTM, not degrees" entries for why that was tried and
 *  reverted — the user wants the raw survey values, not a derived one). */
export interface UtmAxis {
  readonly label: string;
  readonly value: string;
  readonly icon: string;
}

const NUMBER = String.raw`(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)`;
// The "m" is optional so IRs saved before the unit was dropped still split into Northing/Easting/Zone.
const UTM_TEXT = new RegExp(String.raw`^\s*Northing\s+${NUMBER}\s*(?:m\b)?\s*,\s*Easting\s+${NUMBER}\s*(?:m\b)?\s*,\s*Zone\s+(\d{1,2})\s*$`, 'i');

/** Reads a UTM zone field as it actually arrives — a bare number (`38`, what `sv_utm_zn` holds —
 *  Saudi Arabia has no need for a hemisphere letter, every well this app has ever seen is Northern
 *  Hemisphere) or a string that might carry one anyway (`"38N"`, matching `IrEligibleWell.utmZone`'s
 *  declared type). Only the leading digits are read either way. */
export function parseUtmZone(zone: string | number | null | undefined): number | null {
  if (zone === null || zone === undefined) return null;
  const match = /^\s*(\d{1,2})/.exec(String(zone));
  if (!match) return null;
  const zoneNumber = Number(match[1]);
  return zoneNumber >= 1 && zoneNumber <= 60 ? zoneNumber : null;
}

/** Composes the one "coordinates" string this app threads everywhere (the model's single
 *  `requestReference.coordinates` field), straight from the raw survey values — no rounding beyond
 *  what the source numbers already carry. `null` when the zone can't be read, since a northing/
 *  easting pair means nothing without knowing which zone they're measured in. */
export function formatUtmCoordinates(northing: number, easting: number, zone: string | number | null | undefined): string | null {
  const zoneNumber = parseUtmZone(zone);
  if (zoneNumber === null) return null;
  return `Northing ${northing}, Easting ${easting}, Zone ${zoneNumber}`;
}

/** Splits that composed string back into Northing / Easting / UTM Zone for display. Text that
 *  doesn't match (an older record saved before this format, or hand-typed free text) is kept whole
 *  as a single "Coordinates" entry rather than dropped or guessed at. */
export function toUtmAxes(text: string): readonly UtmAxis[] {
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return [];

  const match = UTM_TEXT.exec(trimmed);
  if (!match) {
    return [{ label: 'Coordinates', value: trimmed, icon: 'location_on' }];
  }

  return [
    { label: 'Northing', value: match[1], icon: 'swap_vert' },
    { label: 'Easting', value: match[2], icon: 'swap_horiz' },
    { label: 'UTM Zone', value: match[3], icon: 'public' },
  ];
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-detail-rows.util.ts

```ts
import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { toUtmAxes } from '../wwell-ir-coordinates.util';
import { isLinerCompletionType } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-completion-type.config';

/** Flags a value that's actually meaningful, not just informational — e.g. a nonzero H2S
 *  reading should read as amber/danger regardless of which section it sits in. Omitted (or
 *  `'default'`) renders as a plain field. */
export type RowTone = 'default' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'success';

export interface KvRow {
  readonly label: string;
  readonly value: string;
  readonly tone?: RowTone;
  /** Marks a value picked from a fixed set of options (a completion type, a test type, ...).
   *  A multi-select's picks arrive newline-separated in `value` and are shown one per line. */
  readonly options?: boolean;
  /** An identifier or a measurement (an IR number, a depth, a reading): set in the mono face, like the figures elsewhere. */
  readonly mono?: boolean;
}

export const EMPTY_VALUE = '—';

export function fmtNum(value: number | null | undefined, unit?: string): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return EMPTY_VALUE;
  return unit ? `${value} ${unit}` : `${value}`;
}

export function fmtStr(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  return value ? value : EMPTY_VALUE;
}

/** `linkedRequestId` is the raw prewap_seq (e.g. "5008") — resolve it to the well name. Prefers
 *  `r.wellName` (populated from exad_rcd_prewap on a saved IR, or by the picker on a fresh one)
 *  over the "eligible for a new IR" wellOptions lookup: get_ir_wells_prc.sql's eligibility filter
 *  excludes any well that already has an IR header, so the instant this IR is saved its own
 *  linked well permanently drops out of that list and the lookup can never succeed again — the
 *  wellOptions param only still helps a brand-new, not-yet-saved draft. Falls back to the raw id
 *  if neither source has a name yet, rather than showing nothing. */
function resolveLinkedRequestLabel(
  linkedRequestId: string | null,
  wellName: string,
  wellOptions: readonly { readonly value: string; readonly label: string }[],
): string {
  if (!linkedRequestId) return EMPTY_VALUE;
  return wellName || wellOptions.find(option => option.value === linkedRequestId)?.label || linkedRequestId;
}

/** Splits a "Northing X, Easting Y, Zone Z" coordinate string into its own Northing/Easting/UTM
 *  Zone rows — falls back to a single "Coordinates" row when the text doesn't read as that shape
 *  (unset, or hand-typed free text), same fallback `toUtmAxes` uses for the wellbore panel's
 *  Location card. Shown exactly as the raw survey values, no unit conversion. */
function coordinateKvRows(coordinates: string | null | undefined): readonly KvRow[] {
  if (!coordinates) return [{ label: 'Coordinates', value: EMPTY_VALUE, mono: true }];
  return toUtmAxes(coordinates).map(axis => ({
    label: axis.label,
    value: axis.value,
    mono: true,
  }));
}

/** Mirrors the IR wizard's "Request Reference" step (wwell-ir-form.schema.ts) field-for-field,
 *  so the detail page's sections line up 1:1 with the stepper the data was captured through. */
export function buildRequestReferenceRows(
  ir: IntegratedRequirement,
  linkedRequestWellOptions: readonly { readonly value: string; readonly label: string }[] = [],
): readonly KvRow[] {
  const r = ir.requestReference;
  return [
    { label: 'Link to Request', value: resolveLinkedRequestLabel(r.linkedRequestId, r.wellName, linkedRequestWellOptions) },
    { label: 'Letter Number', value: fmtStr(r.letterNumber), mono: true },
    { label: 'Water Well Name', value: fmtStr(r.wellName) },
    { label: 'Supporting Business', value: fmtStr(r.supportingBusiness) },
    { label: 'Target Aquifer', value: fmtStr(r.targetAquifer), options: true },
    { label: 'BI Number', value: fmtStr(r.client) },
    // A brand-new draft is created through the required-number fallback in
    // mapIrFormValueToSubmission (targetDepthFt ?? 0) before the author has entered a real value —
    // treat a literal 0 as "not yet entered" rather than a misleading real reading (see the
    // matching fallback in mapIntegratedRequirementToFormPatch, wwell-ir-edit.mapper.ts).
    { label: 'Target Depth', value: r.targetDepthFt ? fmtNum(r.targetDepthFt, 'ft') : EMPTY_VALUE, mono: true },
    { label: 'Surface Elevation', value: `${fmtNum(r.surfaceElevationFtAsl)} ft ASL`, mono: true },
    ...coordinateKvRows(r.coordinates),
  ];
}

/** Mirrors the schema's "Program Details" section (2.1) — its own rail leaf/edit unit, separate
 *  from Development & Testing (2.2) below. `openHoleSizeIn` only ever means something when the
 *  completion type includes 'Open Hole' (see the field's `visibleWhen` gate in the schema), so
 *  it's only included then rather than showing a dangling "—" the rest of the time.
 *
 *  `completion` is a multi-select — any number of `WWELL_COMPLETION_OPTIONS` can be checked at
 *  once, each becoming its own `\n`-joined line (see wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper.ts's own
 *  doc comment on this same shape) — so this must check "is 'Open Hole' one of the checked
 *  lines?", the same `includesLine` semantics the schema's `visibleWhen` gate uses
 *  (dynamic-section.component.ts), not a straight equality that only ever matched the
 *  single-selection case and otherwise silently hid an already-entered size. */
export function buildProgramMainRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const p = ir.programDetails;
  const rows: KvRow[] = [{ label: 'Completion', value: fmtStr(p.completionType) }];

  // Every pick is drawn inside the open hole, so its size is listed whatever the pick.
  if (p.completionType) {
    rows.push({ label: 'Open Hole Size', value: fmtStr(p.openHoleSizeIn) });
  }
  if (isLinerCompletionType(p.completionType)) {
    rows.push({ label: 'Liner Screen Size', value: fmtStr(p.linerSizeIn) });
    rows.push({ label: 'Liner Depth (ft)', value: p.linerDepthFt != null ? String(p.linerDepthFt) : 'Target depth' });
  }
  rows.push({ label: 'Completion Remarks', value: fmtStr(p.completion), options: true });

  rows.push(
    { label: 'Acidization', value: fmtStr(p.acidization), options: true },
    { label: 'Transmission', value: fmtStr(p.transmission), options: true },
  );
  return rows;
}

/** Mirrors the schema's "Development & Testing" section (2.2) — see buildProgramMainRows' doc
 *  comment for why this is a separate function rather than one combined list. */
export function buildProgramDevRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const d = ir.programDetails.developmentTesting;
  const isFlowTest = ir.testProgram.testType === 'flow-test';
  return [
    { label: 'Logging', value: fmtStr(d.logging), options: true },
    { label: 'Mud Logging', value: fmtStr(d.mudLogging), options: true },
    { label: 'Sampling', value: fmtStr(d.sampling), options: true },
    // Hydrology Test Type decides which one applies: a flow test shows Flow Test (FLOW_RMKS) only,
    // a pump test shows Development/Testing (DEV_PUMP_TEST) only.
    isFlowTest
      ? { label: 'Flow Test', value: fmtStr(d.flowTest), options: true }
      : { label: 'Development/Testing', value: fmtStr(d.remarks) },
  ];
}

/** Mirrors the wizard's "Estimated Hydrology" step, including its test-type-conditional
 *  fields (SIWHP for a flow test; water level + pump depth for a pump test) — see the
 *  matching `visibleWhen` gates on `siwhpPsi`/`estimatedWaterLevelFt`/`estimatedPumpDepthFt`
 *  in wwell-ir-form.schema.ts. Any nonzero H2S gets `tone: 'warning'` — a toxic-gas reading is
 *  worth flagging on sight, not just listed at the same visual weight as TDS/rate. */
export function buildHydrogeologyRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const isFlowTest = ir.testProgram.testType === 'flow-test';
  const h2sPpm = ir.hydrogeology.h2sPpm;
  const rows: KvRow[] = [
    { label: 'Aquifer', value: fmtStr(ir.hydrogeology.aquifer), options: true },
    { label: 'Test Type', value: isFlowTest ? 'Flow Test' : 'Pump Test', options: true },
    { label: 'Estimated TDS', value: fmtNum(ir.hydrogeology.estimatedTdsPpm, 'ppm'), mono: true },
    { label: 'Estimated Rate', value: fmtNum(ir.testProgram.estimatedRateGpm, 'GPM'), mono: true },
    {
      label: 'H2S',
      value: fmtNum(h2sPpm, 'ppm'),
      tone: h2sPpm ? 'warning' : 'default',
      mono: true,
    },
  ];

  if (isFlowTest) {
    rows.push({ label: 'SIWHP', value: fmtNum(ir.testProgram.siwhpPsi, 'psi'), mono: true });
  } else {
    rows.push(
      { label: 'Estimated Water Level', value: fmtNum(ir.testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt, 'ft'), mono: true },
      { label: 'Estimated Pump Depth', value: fmtNum(ir.hydrogeology.estimatedPumpDepthFt, 'ft'), mono: true },
    );
  }
  return rows;
}

export function buildGeologistContactRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const c = ir.contacts;
  return [
    { label: 'Primary', value: fmtStr(c.geologistPrimary) },
    { label: 'Secondary', value: fmtStr(c.geologistSecondary) },
  ];
}
```

```html
<div class="ire">
  <header class="ire__header">
    <div class="ire__heading">
      <button
        type="button"
        mat-icon-button
        class="ire__back-btn"
        aria-label="Back to list"
        matTooltip="Back to list"
        matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
        (click)="backRequested.emit()"
      >
        <mat-icon>arrow_back</mat-icon>
      </button>
      <div class="ire__heading-text">
        <p class="ire__eyebrow">{{ ir().code }}</p>
        <h2 class="ire__title">{{ headerTitle() }}</h2>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="ire__header-actions">
      <button
        type="button"
        mat-stroked-button
        class="ire__mode-toggle-btn"
        aria-label="Switch to read-only view"
        matTooltip="Switch to read-only view"
        matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
        (click)="viewRequested.emit()"
      >
        <mat-icon>visibility</mat-icon>
        View
      </button>
      @if (hasPendingEdits()) {
        <span class="ire__unsaved-chip" role="status">
          <span class="ire__unsaved-chip-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>
          Unsaved changes — shown in green
        </span>
      }
      <div class="ire__view-toggle" role="group" aria-label="View mode">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="ire__view-toggle-btn"
          [class.ire__view-toggle-btn--active]="viewMode() === 'sections'"
          aria-label="Sections view"
          matTooltip="Sections view"
          matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
          (click)="setViewMode('sections')"
        >
          <mat-icon>view_list</mat-icon>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="ire__view-toggle-btn"
          [class.ire__view-toggle-btn--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline'"
          aria-label="Timeline view"
          matTooltip="Timeline view"
          matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
          (click)="setViewMode('timeline')"
        >
          <mat-icon>timeline</mat-icon>
        </button>
      </div>
      @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
        <button
          type="button"
          mat-icon-button
          class="ire__tl-toggle-all-btn"
          [attr.aria-label]="allTimelineStepsExpanded() ? 'Collapse all sections' : 'Expand all sections'"
          [matTooltip]="allTimelineStepsExpanded() ? 'Collapse all' : 'Expand all'"
          matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
          (click)="toggleAllTimelineSteps()"
        >
          <mat-icon>{{ allTimelineStepsExpanded() ? "unfold_less" : "unfold_more" }}</mat-icon>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          mat-icon-button
          class="ire__tl-toggle-all-btn"
          [class.ire__tl-toggle-all-btn--active]="previewSidebarOpen()"
          [attr.aria-label]="previewSidebarOpen() ? 'Hide wellbore preview' : 'Show wellbore preview'"
          [matTooltip]="previewSidebarOpen() ? 'Hide wellbore preview' : 'Show wellbore preview'"
          matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
          (click)="togglePreviewSidebar()"
        >
          <mat-icon>{{ previewSidebarOpen() ? "view_sidebar" : "dock_to_left" }}</mat-icon>
        </button>
      }
      <!-- The status badge and the approval-workflow indicator used to be two separate chips sitting
           side by side — merged into one control, since both describe the same underlying SAP
           workflow: the badge half is this record's current status, the ring half is how far its
           approval chain has gotten, and clicking either opens the same detail popover. -->
      <button
        #approvalPillOrigin="cdkOverlayOrigin"
        cdkOverlayOrigin
        type="button"
        class="ire__approval-pill"
        [class.ire__approval-pill--muted]="!hasKnownStatus(ir())"
        [style.background]="hasKnownStatus(ir()) ? badgeMeta(ir()).bg : null"
        [style.color]="hasKnownStatus(ir()) ? badgeMeta(ir()).color : null"
        aria-haspopup="true"
        [attr.aria-expanded]="approvalPopoverOpen()"
        aria-label="Status and approval workflow"
        matTooltip="Status and approval workflow"
        matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
        (click)="toggleApprovalPopover()"
      >
        @if (hasKnownStatus(ir())) {
          <span class="ire__approval-pill-dot" [style.background]="badgeMeta(ir()).dot"></span>
          <span class="ire__approval-pill-status">{{ badgeMeta(ir()).label }}</span>
          <span class="ire__approval-pill-divider" aria-hidden="true"></span>
        }
        <svg class="ire__approval-pill-ring" viewBox="0 0 40 40" aria-hidden="true">
          <circle class="ire__approval-pill-ring-track" cx="20" cy="20" r="17" pathLength="100" />
          <circle
            class="ire__approval-pill-ring-fill"
            cx="20"
            cy="20"
            r="17"
            pathLength="100"
            [style.strokeDashoffset]="100 - approvalRingPercent(ir().chain)"
            [style.stroke]="ir().chain.length ? chainCompletionColor(ir().chain) : null"
          />
        </svg>
        <span class="ire__approval-pill-count">
          @if (ir().sapWfId === null) {
            <mat-icon>hourglass_empty</mat-icon>
          } @else {
            {{ countApprovedSteps(ir().chain) }}/{{ ir().chain.length }}
          }
        </span>
        <mat-icon class="ire__approval-pill-chevron">{{ approvalPopoverOpen() ? "expand_less" : "expand_more" }}</mat-icon>
      </button>

      @if (ir().sapWfId !== null) {
        <button
          type="button"
          mat-icon-button
          class="ire__approval-refresh-btn"
          aria-label="Refresh approval status"
          matTooltip="Refresh approval status"
          matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
          [disabled]="store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()"
          (click)="refreshApprovalStatus()"
        >
          <mat-icon>refresh</mat-icon>
        </button>
      }

      <ng-template
        cdkConnectedOverlay
        [cdkConnectedOverlayOrigin]="approvalPillOrigin"
        [cdkConnectedOverlayOpen]="approvalPopoverOpen()"
        [cdkConnectedOverlayHasBackdrop]="true"
        cdkConnectedOverlayBackdropClass="cdk-overlay-transparent-backdrop"
        [cdkConnectedOverlayPositions]="approvalPopoverPositions"
        (backdropClick)="approvalPopoverOpen.set(false)"
        (detach)="approvalPopoverOpen.set(false)"
      >
        <div class="ire__approval-popover">
          <app-wwell-approval-workflow-card
            [ir]="ir()"
            [loading]="store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()"
            (refresh)="refreshApprovalStatus()"
          />
        </div>
      </ng-template>
    </div>
  </header>

  <div class="ire__body" #body [class.ire__body--timeline]="viewMode() === 'timeline'">
    @if (viewMode() === 'sections') {
      <nav class="ire__rail" aria-label="IR sections" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        <p class="ire__rail-title">IR Sections</p>
        <ul class="ire__rail-list">
          @for (group of railGroups; track group.key) {
          <li class="ire__rail-group">
            <button
              type="button"
              class="ire__rail-group-btn"
              [class.ire__rail-group-btn--active]="group.leaf && isLeafActive(group.leaf.key)"
              (click)="scrollToGroupStart(group)"
            >
              <span class="ire__rail-group-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>
              <span class="ire__rail-number">{{ group.number }}</span>
              <span class="ire__rail-label">{{ group.label }}</span>
            </button>
            @if (group.children) {
              <ul class="ire__rail-children">
                @for (child of group.children; track child.key) {
                  <li>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      class="ire__rail-child-btn"
                      [class.ire__rail-child-btn--active]="isLeafActive(child.key)"
                      (click)="scrollToLeaf(child.key)"
                    >
                      <span class="ire__rail-number">{{ child.number }}</span>
                      <span class="ire__rail-label">{{ child.label }}</span>
                    </button>
                  </li>
                }
              </ul>
            }
            </li>
          }
        </ul>
      </nav>
    }

    <div class="ire__sections" [class.ire__sections--timeline]="viewMode() === 'timeline'">
      <!-- 1.0 Water Well Details -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('wellbore') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('wellbore')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('wellbore') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-wellbore" class="ire__section ire__section--wellbore">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('wellbore')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('wellbore')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('wellbore')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">1.0 Water Well Details</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('wellbore') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("wellbore") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
          @if (!requestReferenceEdit.isEditing() && isTimelineStepExpanded('wellbore') && !hasSavedIrSeq()) {
            <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Water Well Details" matTooltip="Edit Water Well Details" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); requestReferenceEdit.open()">
              <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
            </button>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('wellbore')) {
          <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
          @if (requestReferenceEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="referenceMainSection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="requestReferenceEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <dl class="ire__view-grid" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              @for (row of requestReferenceRows(); track row.label) {
                <div class="ire__view-field" [attr.data-tone]="row.tone">
                  <dt>{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd><app-wwell-ir-kv-value [row]="row" /></dd>
                </div>
              }
            </dl>
          }
          </div>
        }
      </section>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 2.0 General -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('general') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('general')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('general') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <div class="ire__group">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('general')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('general')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('general')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">2.0 General</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('general') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("general") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('general')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        <!-- 2.1 Program Details (includes Development & Testing — one combined edit/save unit) -->
        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-program-main" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">2.1 Program Details</h3>
            @if (!programEdit.isEditing()) {
              <div class="ire__section-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Program Details" matTooltip="Edit Program Details" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="programEdit.open()">
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn ire__edit-btn--danger" aria-label="Delete Program Details" matTooltip="Delete Program Details" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="resetProgramSection()">
                  <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (programEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="programSectionForTest()" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="programEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <dl class="ire__view-grid" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              @for (row of programRows(); track row.label) {
                <div class="ire__view-field" [attr.data-tone]="row.tone">
                  <dt>{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd><app-wwell-ir-kv-value [row]="row" /></dd>
                </div>
              }
            </dl>
          }
        </section>

        <!-- 2.2 Geologist Contact -->
        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-geologist" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">2.2 Geologist Contact</h3>
            @if (!geologistContactEdit.isEditing()) {
              <div class="ire__section-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Geologist Contact" matTooltip="Edit Geologist Contact" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="geologistContactEdit.open()">
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn ire__edit-btn--danger" aria-label="Delete Geologist Contact" matTooltip="Delete Geologist Contact" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="resetGeologistSection()">
                  <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (geologistContactEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="geologistSection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="geologistContactEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <dl class="ire__view-grid" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              @for (row of geologistContactRows(); track row.label) {
                <div class="ire__view-field" [attr.data-tone]="row.tone">
                  <dt>{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd><app-wwell-ir-kv-value [row]="row" /></dd>
                </div>
              }
            </dl>
          }
        </section>
        </div>
        }
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 3.0 Horizons -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('horizons') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('horizons')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('horizons') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <div class="ire__group">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('horizons')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('horizons')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('horizons')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">3.0 Horizons</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('horizons') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("horizons") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('horizons')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        <!-- 3.1 Horizons and 3.2 Remarks are independent edit units — opening one's pencil must not
             flip the other into its edit form (see formationTopsEdit's own doc comment in the .ts). -->
        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-horizons" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">3.1 Horizons</h3>
            @if (!formationTopsEdit.visible()) {
              <div class="ire__section-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Add horizon" matTooltip="Add horizon" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="addFormationTopAddRow()">
                  <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Horizons" matTooltip="Edit Horizons" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="formationTopAdd.close(); formationTopsEdit.open()">
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (formationTopAdd.control(); as addCtrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block ire__add-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="formationTopAddSection" [control]="addCtrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="formationTopAdd.close()">Cancel</button>
                <button type="button" mat-flat-button color="primary" [disabled]="addCtrl.invalid || addCtrl.length === 0" (click)="addFormationTopRow()">Add horizon</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          }
```
