```html
          @if (formationTopsEdit.visible() && formationTopsEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="formationTopsSection"
                [control]="ctrl"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="formationTopsEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <div class="ire__view-table-wrap" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <table class="ire__view-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>Formation</th>
                    <th>Depth (ft bgl)</th>
                    <th>Sub-sea (ft asl)</th>
                    <th class="ire__view-table-actions-col">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  @for (top of formationTopsRows(); track top.name; let i = $index) {
                    <tr appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                      @if (formationTopsInlineEdit.isEditing(i) && formationTopsRowGroup(i); as rowGroup) {
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="formationTopsFieldConfig('name')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'name')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="formationTopsFieldConfig('depthFt')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'depthFt')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>{{ subSeaFt(rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'depthFt').value) ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Save formation top" matTooltip="Save formation top" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" [disabled]="rowGroup.invalid" (click)="saveFormationTopRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Cancel formation top edit" matTooltip="Cancel formation top edit" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="cancelFormationTopRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      } @else {
                        <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="topChanged(top, 'name')">{{ top.name }}</td>
                        <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="topChanged(top, 'depthFt')">{{ top.depthFt }}</td>
                        <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="topChanged(top, 'depthFt')">{{ subSeaFt(top.depthFt) ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" [attr.aria-label]="'Edit formation top ' + top.name" [matTooltip]="'Edit formation top ' + top.name" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="editFormationTopRowInline(i)">
                            <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn ire__row-action-btn--danger" [attr.aria-label]="'Delete formation top ' + top.name" [matTooltip]="'Delete formation top ' + top.name" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="deleteFormationTopRow(i)">
                            <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      }
                    </tr>
                  } @empty {
                    <tr>
                      <td colspan="4" class="ire__view-table-empty">No formation tops recorded.</td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          }
        </section>

        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-horizons-remarks" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">3.2 Remarks</h3>
            @if (!topsRemarksEdit.visible()) {
              <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Formation Tops Remarks" matTooltip="Edit Formation Tops Remarks" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="topsRemarksEdit.open()">
                <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
              </button>
            }
          </div>

          @if (topsRemarksEdit.visible() && topsRemarksEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="topsRemarksSection"
                [control]="ctrl"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="topsRemarksEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else if (ir().topsRemarks) {
            <p class="ire__remarks" appWwellCollapseAnimate>{{ ir().topsRemarks }}</p>
          } @else {
            <p class="ire__empty-text" appWwellCollapseAnimate>No remarks.</p>
          }
        </section>
        </div>
        }
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 4.0 Casing -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('casing') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('casing')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('casing') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-casing" class="ire__section">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('casing')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('casing')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('casing')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">4.0 Casing</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('casing') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("casing") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
          @if (!casingEdit.visible() && isTimelineStepExpanded('casing')) {
            <div class="ire__section-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Add casing" matTooltip="Add casing" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); addCasingAddRow()">
                <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
              </button>
              <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Casing" matTooltip="Edit Casing" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); casingAdd.close(); casingEdit.open()">
                <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
              </button>
            </div>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('casing')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        @if (casingAdd.control(); as addCtrl) {
          <div class="ire__edit-block ire__add-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <app-dynamic-section [section]="casingAddSection" [control]="addCtrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
            <div class="ire__edit-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-button (click)="casingAdd.close()">Cancel</button>
              <button type="button" mat-flat-button color="primary" [disabled]="addCtrl.invalid || addCtrl.length === 0" (click)="addCasingRow()">Add casing</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        }
        @if (casingEdit.visible() && casingEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
          <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <app-dynamic-section [section]="casingSection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
            <div class="ire__edit-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-button (click)="casingEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <div class="ire__view-table-wrap" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <table class="ire__view-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Type</th>
                  <th>Size</th>
                  <th>Depth (ft bgl)</th>
                  <th>Remarks</th>
                  <th class="ire__view-table-actions-col">Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                @for (row of casingRows(); track row.type + row.depthFtBgl; let i = $index) {
                  <tr appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                    @if (casingInlineEdit.isEditing(i) && casingRowGroup(i); as rowGroup) {
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('type')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'type')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('sizeIn')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'sizeIn')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('depthFtBgl')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'depthFtBgl')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('remarks')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'remarks')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td class="ire__row-actions">
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Save casing row" matTooltip="Save casing row" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" [disabled]="rowGroup.invalid" (click)="saveCasingRowInline()">
                          <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Cancel casing row edit" matTooltip="Cancel casing row edit" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="cancelCasingRowInline()">
                          <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    } @else {
                      <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="casingChanged(row, 'type')">{{ row.type }}</td>
                      <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="casingChanged(row, 'sizeIn')">{{ row.sizeIn }}</td>
                      <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="casingChanged(row, 'depthFtBgl')">{{ row.depthFtBgl }}</td>
                      <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="casingChanged(row, 'remarks')">{{ row.remarks || "—" }}</td>
                      <td class="ire__row-actions">
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" [attr.aria-label]="'Edit casing row ' + (i + 1)" [matTooltip]="'Edit casing row ' + (i + 1)" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="editCasingRowInline(i)">
                          <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn ire__row-action-btn--danger" [attr.aria-label]="'Delete casing row ' + (i + 1)" [matTooltip]="'Delete casing row ' + (i + 1)" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="deleteCasingRow(i)">
                          <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    }
                  </tr>
                } @empty {
                  <tr>
                    <td colspan="5" class="ire__view-table-empty">No casing recorded.</td>
                  </tr>
                }
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          @if (casingSizeWarnings().length) {
            <ul class="ire__casing-warnings">
              @for (warning of casingSizeWarnings(); track warning.rowIndex) {
                <li class="ire__casing-warning">
                  <mat-icon>warning</mat-icon>
                  <span>
                    Casing #{{ warning.rowIndex + 1 }} ({{ warning.size }}) is deeper and wider than
                    Casing #{{ warning.conflictsWithRowIndex + 1 }} ({{ warning.conflictsWithSize }}) — the
                    diagram will draw it over that shallower casing, which may hide it.
                  </span>
                </li>
              }
            </ul>
          }
        }
        </div>
        }
      </section>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Wellbore preview — read-only, always derived from the persisted record. Sections view
           keeps it inline right here between Casing and Hydrogeology, unchanged. Timeline view
           doesn't render it inline at all — it isn't tied to any one lifecycle step, so it's
           pinned in a sticky sidebar instead (see `wellborePreviewTpl` usage further down),
           staying visible no matter which step is open or how far the steps are scrolled. -->
      @if (viewMode() === 'sections') {
        <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="wellborePreviewTpl" />
      }

      <ng-template #wellborePreviewTpl let-compact="compact">
        <app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel
          [diagramData]="wellboreDiagramData()"
          [hasData]="hasWellboreData()"
          [hasTargetDepth]="hasTargetDepth()"
          [compact]="!!compact"
          [irCode]="ir().code"
          [coordinates]="headerCoordinates()"
          [testType]="headerTestType()"
          [layout]="compact ? sidebarDiagramLayout : previewDiagramLayout"
        />
      </ng-template>

      <!-- 5.0 Hydrogeology -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('hydrogeology') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('hydrogeology')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('hydrogeology') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-hydrogeology" class="ire__section">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('hydrogeology')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('hydrogeology')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('hydrogeology')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">5.0 Hydrogeology</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('hydrogeology') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("hydrogeology") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
          @if (!hydrogeologyEdit.isEditing() && isTimelineStepExpanded('hydrogeology')) {
            <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Hydrogeology" matTooltip="Edit Hydrogeology" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); hydrogeologyEdit.open()">
              <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
            </button>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('hydrogeology')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        @if (hydrogeologyEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
          <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <app-dynamic-section [section]="hydrogeologySection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
            <div class="ire__edit-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-button (click)="hydrogeologyEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <dl class="ire__view-grid" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            @for (row of hydrogeologyRows(); track row.label) {
              <div class="ire__view-field" [attr.data-tone]="row.tone">
                <dt>{{ row.label }}</dt>
                <dd><app-wwell-ir-kv-value [row]="row" /></dd>
              </div>
            }
          </dl>
        }
        </div>
        }
      </section>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 6.0 Area Water Wells -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('areaWaterWells') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('areaWaterWells')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('areaWaterWells') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <div class="ire__group">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('areaWaterWells')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('areaWaterWells')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('areaWaterWells')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">6.0 Area Water Wells</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('areaWaterWells') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("areaWaterWells") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('areaWaterWells')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        <!-- 6.1 / 6.2 Area Water Wells + Remarks — one shared edit unit, matching the domain model (offsetWellsRemarks lives beside offsetWells on the same record). -->
        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-offset-wells" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">6.1 Area Water Wells</h3>
            @if (!offsetWellsEdit.visible()) {
              <div class="ire__section-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Add area water well" matTooltip="Add area water well" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="addOffsetWellAddRow()">
                  <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Area Water Wells" matTooltip="Edit Area Water Wells" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="offsetWellAdd.close(); offsetWellsEdit.open()">
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (offsetWellAdd.control(); as addCtrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block ire__add-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="offsetWellAddSection" [control]="addCtrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="offsetWellAdd.close()">Cancel</button>
                <button type="button" mat-flat-button color="primary" [disabled]="addCtrl.invalid || addCtrl.length === 0" (click)="addOffsetWellRow()">Add well</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          }

          @if (offsetWellsEdit.visible() && offsetWellsEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="offsetWellsSection"
                [control]="ctrl"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
            </div>
          } @else {
            <div class="ire__view-table-wrap" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <table class="ire__view-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>EP A Num</th>
                    <th>Aquifer</th>
                    <th>Distance (km)</th>
                    <th>Rate (GPM)</th>
                    <th class="ire__view-table-actions-col">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  @for (row of offsetWellsRows(); track row.epANum; let i = $index) {
                    <tr appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                      @if (offsetWellsInlineEdit.isEditing(i) && offsetWellsRowGroup(i); as rowGroup) {
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('epANum')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'epANum')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('aquifer')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'aquifer')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('distanceKm')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'distanceKm')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('rateGpm')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'rateGpm')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Save offset well" matTooltip="Save offset well" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" [disabled]="rowGroup.invalid" (click)="saveOffsetWellRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Cancel offset well edit" matTooltip="Cancel offset well edit" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="cancelOffsetWellRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      } @else {
                        <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="wellChanged(row, 'epANum')">{{ row.epANum }}</td>
                        <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="wellChanged(row, 'aquifer')">{{ row.aquifer || "—" }}</td>
                        <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="wellChanged(row, 'distanceKm')">{{ row.distanceKm ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td [class.ire__cell--changed]="wellChanged(row, 'rateGpm')">{{ row.rateGpm ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" [attr.aria-label]="'Edit offset well ' + row.epANum" [matTooltip]="'Edit offset well ' + row.epANum" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="editOffsetWellRowInline(i)">
                            <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn ire__row-action-btn--danger" [attr.aria-label]="'Delete offset well ' + row.epANum" [matTooltip]="'Delete offset well ' + row.epANum" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="deleteOffsetWellRow(i)">
                            <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      }
                    </tr>
                  } @empty {
                    <tr>
                      <td colspan="5" class="ire__view-table-empty">No offset wells recorded.</td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          }
        </section>

        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-offset-remarks" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">6.2 Remarks</h3>
            @if (!offsetRemarksEdit.visible()) {
              <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Area Water Wells Remarks" matTooltip="Edit Area Water Wells Remarks" matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip" (click)="offsetRemarksEdit.open()">
                <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
              </button>
            }
          </div>

          @if (offsetRemarksEdit.visible() && offsetRemarksEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="offsetRemarksSection"
                [control]="ctrl"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="offsetRemarksEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else if (ir().offsetWellsRemarks) {
            <p class="ire__remarks" appWwellCollapseAnimate>{{ ir().offsetWellsRemarks }}</p>
          } @else {
            <p class="ire__empty-text" appWwellCollapseAnimate>No remarks.</p>
          }
        </section>
        </div>
        }
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 7.0 Attachments -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('attachments') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('attachments')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('attachments') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <section class="ire__section">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('attachments')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('attachments')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('attachments')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">7.0 Attachments</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('attachments') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("attachments") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('attachments')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>

        @if (store.attachmentError(); as error) {
          <p class="ire__attachment-error" role="alert" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <mat-icon>error_outline</mat-icon>
            {{ error }}
          </p>
        }

        <!-- One #sectionEl per slot, index-aligned with the 7.1-7.3 rail leaves (see
             wwell-ir-section-rail.config.ts) exactly as 6.1/6.2 above are. A slot is a list, not a
             single file — any number of documents can be attached to it, each its own row with its
             own independent View/Upload/Delete and its own progress bar. -->
        @for (slot of attachmentSlots; track slot.slot) {
          <section #sectionEl [id]="'ire-sec-' + slot.slot" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
            <div class="ire__section-head">
              <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">
                <mat-icon class="ire__attachment-slot-icon">{{ slot.icon }}</mat-icon>
                {{ slot.number }} {{ slot.label }}
                @if (slot.required) {
                  <span class="ire__attachment-required" aria-hidden="true">*</span>
                }
              </h3>
              <!-- Always visible — attaching never replaces what's already here, only adds to it,
                   so there's no "occupied" state that would hide this the way a single-file slot
                   used to. A <label> wrapping a hidden input, not a <button>: this opens the OS
                   file picker natively, so there's no click-forwarding JS to keep working. -->
              <label
                class="ire__attachment-add-btn"
                [attr.aria-label]="'Attach ' + slot.label"
                matTooltip="Attach a file"
                matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
                matTooltipPosition="above"
              >
                <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
                <input
                  type="file"
                  hidden
                  [accept]="acceptedAttachmentTypes"
                  (change)="onAttachmentPicked(slot.slot, $event)"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            @if (attachmentsFor(slot.slot).length || pendingAttachmentsFor(slot.slot).length) {
              <ul class="ire__attachment-list">
                @for (attachment of attachmentsFor(slot.slot); track attachment.objectKey) {
                  <li class="ire__attachment-file" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                    <mat-icon class="ire__attachment-file-icon">{{ attachmentIcon(attachment.contentType) }}</mat-icon>
                    <span class="ire__attachment-file-meta">
                      <span class="ire__attachment-file-name">{{ attachment.fileName }}</span>
                      <span class="ire__attachment-file-size">{{ attachmentSizeLabel(attachment.sizeBytes) }}</span>
                    </span>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      mat-icon-button
                      class="ire__attachment-action"
                      [attr.aria-label]="'View ' + attachment.fileName"
                      matTooltip="View"
                      matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
                      matTooltipPosition="above"
                      (click)="viewStoredAttachment(attachment, slot.slot)"
                    >
                      <mat-icon>visibility</mat-icon>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      mat-icon-button
                      class="ire__attachment-action ire__attachment-action--danger"
                      [attr.aria-label]="'Remove ' + attachment.fileName"
                      matTooltip="Delete"
                      matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
                      matTooltipPosition="above"
                      [disabled]="isStoredAttachmentBusy(attachment.objectKey)"
                      (click)="deleteStoredAttachment(attachment)"
                    >
                      <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                    </button>

                    @if (isStoredAttachmentBusy(attachment.objectKey)) {
                      <mat-progress-bar class="ire__attachment-progress" mode="indeterminate" />
                    }
                  </li>
                }

                @for (item of pendingAttachmentsFor(slot.slot); track item.id) {
                  <li class="ire__attachment-file ire__attachment-file--pending" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                    <mat-icon class="ire__attachment-file-icon">{{ attachmentIcon(item.file.type) }}</mat-icon>
                    <span class="ire__attachment-file-meta">
                      <span class="ire__attachment-file-name">{{ item.file.name }}</span>
                      <span class="ire__attachment-file-size">
                        {{ attachmentSizeLabel(item.file.size) }} · uploads when the IR is saved
                      </span>
                    </span>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      mat-icon-button
                      class="ire__attachment-action"
                      [attr.aria-label]="'View ' + item.file.name"
                      matTooltip="View"
                      matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
                      matTooltipPosition="above"
                      (click)="viewPendingAttachment(item, slot.slot)"
                    >
                      <mat-icon>visibility</mat-icon>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      mat-icon-button
                      class="ire__attachment-action ire__attachment-action--danger"
                      [attr.aria-label]="'Remove ' + item.file.name"
                      matTooltip="Delete"
                      matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
                      matTooltipPosition="above"
                      [disabled]="isPendingUploadBusy(item.id)"
                      (click)="removePendingAttachment(slot.slot, item)"
                    >
                      <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                    </button>

                    @if (isPendingUploadBusy(item.id)) {
                      <mat-progress-bar
                        class="ire__attachment-progress"
                        [mode]="uploadPercentFor(item.id) === null ? 'indeterminate' : 'determinate'"
                        [value]="uploadPercentFor(item.id) ?? 0"
                      />
                    }
                  </li>
                }
              </ul>
            } @else {
              <p class="ire__attachment-empty-hint" appWwellCollapseAnimate>{{ slot.hint }} PDF, JPG or PNG · max 25 MB</p>
            }
          </section>
        }
        </div>
        }
      </section>
      </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Stays in the DOM for all of Timeline view (not gated on previewSidebarOpen) so hiding it
         is a pure CSS transition (see --hidden below) instead of an instant destroy/recreate. -->
    @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
      <aside class="ire__tl-preview-sidebar" [class.ire__tl-preview-sidebar--hidden]="!previewSidebarOpen()">
        <div class="ire__tl-preview-anim">
          <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="wellborePreviewTpl" [ngTemplateOutletContext]="{ compact: true }" />
        </div>
      </aside>
    }
  </div>

  <footer class="ire__footer">
    <button
      type="button"
      mat-stroked-button
      color="primary"
      class="ire__save-btn"
      [disabled]="anyFooterActionPending()"
      (click)="saveAll()"
    >
      @if (isSaving()) {
        <mat-spinner diameter="16" class="ire__save-spinner" />
      } @else {
        <mat-icon>save</mat-icon>
      }
      Save
    </button>
    @if (canSubmitForReview()) {
      <button
        type="button"
        mat-flat-button
        color="primary"
        class="ire__action-btn"
        [disabled]="anyFooterActionPending() || hasPendingEdits()"
        [attr.title]="hasPendingEdits() ? 'Save your changes before submitting for review' : null"
        (click)="submitForReview()"
      >
        @if (isSubmittingForReview()) {
          <mat-spinner diameter="16" class="ire__save-spinner" />
        } @else {
          <mat-icon>send</mat-icon>
        }
        Submit for Review
      </button>
    } @else if (canRecallIr()) {
      <button
        type="button"
        mat-stroked-button
        color="warn"
        class="ire__action-btn"
        [disabled]="anyFooterActionPending()"
        (click)="recallIr()"
      >
        @if (isRecalling()) {
          <mat-spinner diameter="16" class="ire__save-spinner" />
        } @else {
          <mat-icon>undo</mat-icon>
        }
        Recall
      </button>
    }
  </footer>
</div>
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-editor.component.scss

```scss
@use '@angular/material' as mat;
@use '../../shared/wwell-select-panel-mixin' as wsel;
@use '../../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;
@use '../../shared/wwell-material' as wmat;

$navy: tokens.$navy-deep;
$muted: tokens.$muted;
$border: tokens.$border;
$accent: tokens.$accent;
$warning: tokens.$warning;

// `:host` is a no-op under ViewEncapsulation.None (see the component's own comment) — the tag
// selector is what actually reaches this element. Bounds the component's own height end to end
// so only `.ire__body` scrolls internally; header/rail-label/footer stay fixed chrome, same
// pattern as app-wwell-ir-detail (wwell-ir-detail.component.scss).
app-wwell-ir-editor {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  min-height: 0;
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
}

// Same rationale as wwell-ir-new-page/wwell-ir-detail's identical block — the app only calls
// mat.core() globally with no full M3 theme, so every Material control here (selects, inputs,
// toggle groups) needs its own scoped theme, including a select's portaled overlay panel (CDK
// renders it into <body>, hence the separate `.ire-select-panel` target).
.ire,
.ire-select-panel {
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);

  @include mat.theme(
    (
      color: (
        theme-type: color-scheme,
        primary: mat.$blue-palette,
        tertiary: mat.$azure-palette,
      ),
      typography: (
        plain-family: tokens.$font-family,
        brand-family: tokens.$font-family,
      ),
      density: -4,
    )
  );
}

.ire {
  @include wmat.buttons;
  @include wmat.form-fields;
}

.ire-select-panel {
  border-radius: 10px !important;
  box-shadow:
    var(--wp-shadow-3) !important;
  @include wsel.panel($accent, $navy);
}

.ire,
.ire *,
.ire *::before,
.ire *::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.ire {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  gap: var(--wwell-gutter, 0.6rem);
  padding: 0;
  @include wsel.trigger($accent);
}

/* ── Header: fixed chrome, never scrolls ─────────────────────────────────── */
.ire__header {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.7rem 1.1rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
}

.ire__heading {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.65rem;
  min-width: 0;
}

.ire__heading-text {
  min-width: 0;
}

.ire__back-btn {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0;
  line-height: 32px;
  color: $muted;
  border-radius: 999px;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  ::ng-deep .mat-icon {
    font-size: 20px;
    width: 20px;
    height: 20px;
    line-height: 20px;
  }

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 8%);
  }
}

.ire__mode-toggle-btn {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;

  mat-icon {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    font-size: 16px;
    margin-right: 2px;
  }
}

.ire__eyebrow {
  margin: 0 0 2px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: $accent;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.ire__title {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-lg;
  font-weight: 650;
  line-height: 1.2;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.ire__header-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

/* ── Approval-workflow pill — merges what used to be two adjacent chips (the plain status badge,
 * and this ring+count control) into one: the dot+label half is `badgeMeta(ir())` (same colours the
 * list row's own badge uses), the ring+count half is the same progress math
 * WwellApprovalWorkflowCardComponent uses for its own ring (pathLength="100" normalizes
 * stroke-dashoffset to a plain 0–100). Both halves open the same popover on click, since they
 * describe the same underlying SAP workflow. Every element inside reads `currentColor` off the
 * button's own inline `color` (set to `badgeMeta(ir()).color` in the template when known), so the
 * whole pill recolours as one unit instead of the ring and the label drifting out of sync. */
.ire__approval-pill {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 3px 12px 3px 10px;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  color: $accent;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: box-shadow 150ms ease;

  &:hover {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 999px rgb(0 0 0 / 4%);
  }

  &[aria-expanded='true'] {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1.5px currentColor;
  }

  &--muted:hover {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px tokens.alpha($accent, 35%);
  }
}

.ire__approval-pill-dot {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__approval-pill-status {
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 650;
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: currentColor;
}

// A hairline tick between the status half and the ring+count half — cheaper than a border and
// scales with the pill's own (dynamic, inline) text colour instead of a fixed grey that would
// clash against a saturated background.
.ire__approval-pill-divider {
  width: 1px;
  align-self: stretch;
  margin: 3px 0;
  background: currentColor;
  opacity: 0.25;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__approval-pill-ring {
  width: 20px;
  height: 20px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  // Starts the fill at 12 o'clock instead of SVG's default 3 o'clock.
  transform: rotate(-90deg);
}

.ire__approval-pill-ring-track {
  fill: none;
  stroke: currentColor;
  opacity: 0.25;
  stroke-width: 4;
}

.ire__approval-pill-ring-fill {
  fill: none;
  stroke: currentColor;
  stroke-width: 4;
  stroke-linecap: round;
  stroke-dasharray: 100;
  transition: stroke-dashoffset tokens.$duration-slow tokens.$ease-out;
}

.ire__approval-pill-count {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: currentColor;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

  mat-icon {
    width: 14px;
    height: 14px;
    font-size: 14px;
    color: currentColor;
    opacity: 0.75;
  }
}

.ire__approval-pill-chevron {
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
  font-size: 16px;
  color: currentColor;
  opacity: 0.6;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ire__approval-pill-ring-fill {
    transition: none;
  }
}

/* A second, always-visible way to refresh — the popover's own card keeps its own refresh button
 * too (see WwellApprovalWorkflowCardComponent), but that one only exists once the popover is
 * already open; this one works without opening it first. */
.ire__approval-refresh-btn {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  padding: 0;
  line-height: 28px;
  color: $muted;

  ::ng-deep .mat-icon {
    font-size: 18px;
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    line-height: 18px;
  }

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    color: $accent;
  }
}

/* The overlay pane itself has no size/background of its own — this inner box just bounds
 * WwellApprovalWorkflowCardComponent to a sane popover size; the card supplies its own padding,
 * glass background and shadow (same component the sidebar uses, unstyled here). */
.ire__approval-popover {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  width: 280px;
  max-height: min(70vh, 480px);

  app-wwell-approval-workflow-card {
    flex: 1;
    min-height: 0;
  }
}

/* ── Sections/Timeline view toggle ────────────────────────────────
 * A plain segmented control — no @angular/material/button-toggle import needed for two states. */
.ire__view-toggle {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 2px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 999px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__view-toggle-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  border: none;
  background: none;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 0;
  color: $muted;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  mat-icon {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    font-size: 16px;
  }

  &:hover {
    color: $navy;
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid $accent;
    outline-offset: 1px;
  }
}

.ire__view-toggle-btn--active {
  background: tokens.$surface;
  color: $accent;
  box-shadow: var(--wp-shadow-1);

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
  }
}

.ire__tl-toggle-all-btn {
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  color: $muted;

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    font-size: 18px;
  }
}

.ire__tl-toggle-all-btn--active {
  color: $accent;
  background: tokens.$accent-soft;
  border-radius: 50%;
}

/* ── Body: IR SECTIONS rail + the always-rendered form ───────────────────── */
.ire__body {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: var(--wwell-gutter, 0.7rem);
  overflow-y: auto;
  overscroll-behavior-y: contain;
}

/* ── Rail ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * A plain nested nav list, not the old wizard's absolutely-positioned chip timeline — nothing
 * here needs to track a panel's live Y-position (see SectionRailController's doc comment: every
 * section is always rendered, so there's no accordion resize to chase). `position: sticky`
 * against `.ire__body` (its scrolling ancestor) is enough to keep it in view.
 */
.ire__rail {
  // One vertical rhythm for the whole nav. Every row is the same height and every consecutive
  // pair is separated by the same gap, whether or not a group boundary falls between them —
  // previously the gap ran 0 / 0.8 / 2.4 / 5.6px depending on where you were in the tree, which
  // fused each group's children together and opened a void before the next group. Hierarchy is
  // carried by indent, the connector line and type weight instead.
  --rail-gap: 0.15rem;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  align-self: flex-start;
  width: 196px;
  max-height: 100%;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 0.65rem 0.5rem;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  box-shadow:
    var(--wp-shadow-1);
}

.ire__rail-title {
  margin: 0 0 0.4rem 0.4rem; // matches a row's 0.4rem content inset (3px border + padding)
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 650;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: $muted;
}

.ire__rail-list,
.ire__rail-children {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: var(--rail-gap);
}

.ire__rail-group-btn,
.ire__rail-child-btn {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  width: 100%;
  border: none;
  border-left: 3px solid transparent;
  background: none;
  border-radius: 7px;
  padding: 0.32rem 0.4rem 0.32rem calc(0.4rem - 3px);
  font: inherit;
  text-align: left;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: $navy;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    border-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  &:hover {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 8%);
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid $accent;
    outline-offset: -2px;
  }
}

.ire__rail-group-dot {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: $muted;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__rail-number {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  font-weight: 650;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  color: $muted;
}

.ire__rail-label {
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ire__rail-group-btn--active,
.ire__rail-child-btn--active {
  background: tokens.alpha($accent, 12%);
  border-left-color: $accent;

  .ire__rail-number,
  .ire__rail-label {
    color: $accent;
  }

  .ire__rail-group-dot {
    background: $accent;
  }
}

.ire__rail-children {
  // Top margin matches the list gap so stepping from a group header into its first child costs
  // the same as any other step; no bottom margin, so the parent list's own gap alone separates
  // the last child from the next group.
  margin: var(--rail-gap) 0 0 1.3rem;
  padding-left: 0.4rem;
  border-left: 1px solid $border;
}

.ire__rail-child-btn {
  // No padding override: the shared rule above already carries the row height and the
  // `calc(0.4rem - 3px)` that compensates for the transparent 3px active-marker border. The
  // shorthand that used to sit here dropped that compensation, pushing every child's text 3px
  // off the indent grid, and made child rows 2.5px shorter than group rows.
  .ire__rail-label {
    font-weight: 500;
  }
}

/* ── Sections column ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.ire__sections {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.65rem;
  padding-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.ire__section,
.ire__group {
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.7rem 0.9rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
  // Keeps a scrolled-to section's heading clear of the sticky app chrome above `.ire__body`.
  scroll-margin-top: 0.5rem;
}

.ire__group {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0;
}

.ire__group .ire__section--sub {
  border: none;
  border-radius: 0;
  // Inherited from `.ire__section` above; a sub-section is only a divided region of its group,
  // so its own shadow drew a faint box edge around each one.
  box-shadow: none;
  background: transparent;
  border-top: 1px solid $border;
  margin: 0.65rem 0 0;
  padding: 0.65rem 0 0;

  &:first-of-type {
    margin-top: 0.5rem;
    border-top: none;
    padding-top: 0;
  }
}

.ire__section-title {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  position: relative;
  margin: 0 0 0.5rem;
  padding-left: 0.65rem;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 0.1em;
    bottom: 0.1em;
    width: 3px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: $accent;
  }
}

.ire__group > .ire__section-head {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ── Timeline view ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * `.ire__tl-row`/`.ire__tl-rail`/`.ire__tl-content` wrap every top-level section unconditionally
 * in the template (see wwell-ir-editor.component.html) so switching view modes never re-creates
 * a section's SectionEditState or its scroll-spy anchor. In "sections" mode (the default) they
 * collapse to `display: contents` — invisible to layout — so today's plain `.ire__sections` flex
 * list renders exactly as before. Only `.ire__sections--timeline` turns them into an actual
 * dot + connecting-line stepper, using the same light-surface tokens as the rest of the page. */
.ire__tl-row,
.ire__tl-content {
  display: contents;
}

.ire__tl-rail {
  display: none;
}

.ire__body--timeline {
  align-items: stretch;
}

.ire__sections--timeline {
  padding-left: 0.1rem;

  .ire__tl-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 0.65rem;
  }

  .ire__tl-rail {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    width: 22px;
    flex-shrink: 0;

    &::after {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: 22px;
      bottom: -0.65rem;
      left: 50%;
      width: 2px;
      background: $border;
      transform: translateX(-50%);
    }
  }

  .ire__tl-row:last-child .ire__tl-rail::after {
    display: none;
  }

  .ire__tl-content {
    display: block;
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
  }

  .ire__section-head,
  .ire__group > .ire__section-head {
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    border-radius: 4px;

    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid $accent;
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }
  }
}

/* ── Timeline preview panel ────────────────────────────────────────
 * The Wellbore Preview isn't tied to any one lifecycle step, so Timeline view pins it here
 * instead of leaving it in the scrolling step list — `position: sticky` against `.ire__body`
 * (its scrolling ancestor), same pattern the Sections rail already uses, so it stays in view no
 * matter which step is open or how far the steps are scrolled. It's a normal flex sibling of the
 * steps column, never `position: fixed` — it must never sit as a floating overlay on top of the
 * steps content, only ever occupy its own reserved width alongside it.
 *
 * Hiding it animates that reserved `width` down to 0 (rather than removing it from the DOM, or
 * just fading it in place) specifically so the steps column visibly reclaims the freed space as
 * it collapses, instead of leaving a blank gap where the panel used to be. The inner
 * `.ire__tl-preview-anim` wrapper holds its own width fixed and fades/slides out on a faster,
 * independent transition, so the content is already gone by the time the outer collapse would
 * otherwise start clipping it mid-shape.
 */
.ire__tl-preview-sidebar {
  // Grows with the page (the diagram, its figures and labels scale with it), between the old fixed 320px and 27rem.
  --ire-preview-w: clamp(20rem, 27vw, 27rem);

  flex-shrink: 0;
  // A column, so the card inside can be told to fill exactly what the page leaves it (a percentage height would
  // have nothing definite to resolve against here, which is what let the card run past the fold and get clipped).
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  align-self: flex-start;
  width: var(--ire-preview-w);
  // As tall as the page leaves it, whatever the card inside is: the card is told to fill this, so a height taken
  // from the card's own content would be circular (and used to let the card run past the fold and get clipped).
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  transition: width tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out;

  &.ire__tl-preview-sidebar--hidden {
    width: 0;
  }
}

.ire__tl-preview-anim {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  width: var(--ire-preview-w, 20rem);
  overflow-y: auto;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  transition:
    opacity tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
}

.ire__tl-preview-sidebar--hidden .ire__tl-preview-anim {
  opacity: 0;
  transform: translateX(16px);
}

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  // Only the resting (open) width shrinks here — the hidden-state override above always collapses
  // to 0 regardless of breakpoint, so it isn't repeated in this media query.
  .ire__tl-preview-sidebar {
    --ire-preview-w: 260px;
  }
}

.ire__tl-dot {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  margin-top: 0.1rem;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 2px solid $border;
  flex-shrink: 0;

  mat-icon {
    width: 13px;
    height: 13px;
    font-size: 13px;
    color: tokens.$surface;
  }
}

.ire__tl-dot--done {
  background: var(--wp-success-fill);
  border-color: var(--wp-success-fill);
}

.ire__tl-dot--active {
  border-color: $accent;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px tokens.alpha($accent, 16%);
}

// The cobalt ring marks the step still to do. Timeline view opens every step, so a completed one is
// also "active" — without this it took the active ring and halo over its green fill and read as
// half-done. Done wins: green fill, green ring, green halo.
.ire__tl-dot--done.ire__tl-dot--active {
  border-color: var(--wp-success-fill);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px tokens.alpha(var(--wp-success-fill), 22%);
}

.ire__tl-status {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $muted;
  padding: 0.1rem 0.6rem;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ire__tl-status--done {
  color: var(--wp-success);
  background: var(--wp-success-soft);
  border-color: var(--wp-success-line);
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-editor.component.spec.ts

```ts
import { signal } from '@angular/core';
import { ComponentFixture, TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { NoopAnimationsModule } from '@angular/platform-browser/animations';
import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { NotificationService } from '@shared/components/notification/notification.service';
import { S3Service } from '@services/s3/s3.service';
import { WwellIrStore } from '../store/wwell-ir.store';
import { WwellIrEditorComponent } from './wwell-ir-editor.component';
import { WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL, wwellIrRailLeaves } from './wwell-ir-section-rail.config';

function buildIr(overrides: Partial<IntegratedRequirement> = {}): IntegratedRequirement {
  return {
    id: 'IR-9001',
    code: 'IR-9001',
    irSeq: 9001,
    updatedDate: '2026-08-01',
    status: 'draft',
    requestReference: {
      linkedRequestId: '5001',
      letterNumber: 'LTR-2026-0456',
      client: 'BI-12',
      supportingBusiness: 'Agriculture',
      wellName: 'HAIL-500',
      targetAquifer: 'Saq',
      targetDepthFt: 500,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: 900,
      coordinates: '27.5114°N, 41.7208°E',
    },
    formationTops: [],
    topsRemarks: null,
    casing: [],
    programDetails: {
      completionType: '',
      completion: 'Open Hole',
      openHoleSizeIn: null,
      linerSizeIn: null, linerDepthFt: null,
      acidization: '',
      transmission: '',
      developmentTesting: { logging: '', mudLogging: '', sampling: '', flowTest: '', remarks: '' },
    },
    contacts: { geologistPrimary: 'Eng. A. Al-Mutairi', geologistSecondary: null },
    offsetWells: [],
    offsetWellsRemarks: null,
    hydrogeology: { aquifer: 'Saq', estimatedTdsPpm: null, h2sPpm: null, estimatedPumpDepthFt: null },
    testProgram: { testType: 'flow-test', estimatedRateGpm: null, estimatedWaterLevelFt: null, siwhpPsi: null },
    attachments: [
      {
        slot: 'location-map',
        objectKey: 'ir-attachments/ir-9001/location-map/test-uuid',
        label: 'Location Map',
        fileName: 'location-map.pdf',
        sizeBytes: 1024,
        contentType: 'application/pdf',
      },
      {
        slot: 'initiation-request',
        objectKey: 'ir-attachments/ir-9001/initiation-request/test-uuid',
        label: 'Initiation Request',
        fileName: 'initiation-request.pdf',
        sizeBytes: 1024,
        contentType: 'application/pdf',
      },
      {
        slot: 'wlda',
        objectKey: 'ir-attachments/ir-9001/wlda/test-uuid',
        label: 'Well Location and Drilling Approval (WLDA)',
        fileName: 'wlda.pdf',
        sizeBytes: 1024,
        contentType: 'application/pdf',
      },
    ],
    chain: [],
    sapWfId: null,
    sapWfStatusText: null,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

const creatingIrSignal = signal(false);
const createIrErrorSignal = signal<string | null>(null);
const deletingIrSignal = signal(false);
const updatingIrStatusSignal = signal(false);
const recallingIrSignal = signal(false);

const storeMock = {
  creatingIr: creatingIrSignal,
  createIrError: createIrErrorSignal,
  createIr: jest.fn(),
  submitIrForReview: jest.fn(),
  deletingIr: deletingIrSignal,
  deleteIr: jest.fn(),
  updatingIrStatus: updatingIrStatusSignal,
  recallingIr: recallingIrSignal,
  recallIr: jest.fn(),
  irApprovalTimelineLoading: jest.fn(() => false),
  refreshIrApprovalStatus: jest.fn(),
  linkedRequestWells: jest.fn(() => [] as import('../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.model').IrEligibleWell[]),
  linkedRequestWellOptions: jest.fn(() => [] as { value: string; label: string }[]),
  loadLinkedRequestWells: jest.fn(),
  geologistOptions: jest.fn(() => [] as string[]),
  geologistEmpNumByName: jest.fn(() => ({}) as Readonly<Record<string, number>>),
  loadGwdOpsMembers: jest.fn(),
  uploadingPendingIds: jest.fn(() => new Set<string>()),
  uploadProgressByPendingId: jest.fn(() => ({}) as Record<string, number | null>),
  deletingAttachmentKeys: jest.fn(() => new Set<string>()),
  attachmentError: jest.fn(() => null),
  pendingAttachments: jest.fn(
    () => ({}) as Partial<Record<string, readonly import('@models/wwell-planning/ir-attachment.model').IrPendingAttachment[]>>,
  ),
  draftIrSeq: jest.fn(() => null as number | null),
  deleteIrAttachment: jest.fn(),
  addPendingAttachment: jest.fn(),
  removePendingAttachment: jest.fn(),
  uploadPendingAttachment: jest.fn(),
  uploadPendingAttachments: jest.fn(),
};

/** The editor only reaches S3Service directly for a stored attachment's download/view — the
 *  uploads/deletes themselves go through the store (mocked above), so this is all the surface the
 *  component actually uses. */
const s3ServiceMock = {
  download: jest.fn(),
};

const notificationServiceMock = {
  show: jest.fn(),
  info: jest.fn(),
  error: jest.fn(),
  warn: jest.fn(),
  notifications: jest.fn(() => []),
  dismiss: jest.fn(),
};

function findButtonByText(fixture: ComponentFixture<WwellIrEditorComponent>, text: string): HTMLButtonElement | undefined {
  return Array.from(fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('button')).find((btn): btn is HTMLButtonElement =>
    (btn as HTMLButtonElement).textContent?.includes(text) ?? false,
  );
}

/** Scoped to `.ire__footer` specifically for the Submit for Review / Recall assertions below —
 *  the header's approval pill now shows the record's status label inline (e.g. "Recalled"), and a
 *  plain fixture-wide `includes('Recall')` search would false-positive match that label's own
 *  substring instead of the real footer action button. */
function findFooterButtonByText(fixture: ComponentFixture<WwellIrEditorComponent>, text: string): HTMLButtonElement | undefined {
  return Array.from(fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('.ire__footer button')).find((btn): btn is HTMLButtonElement =>
    (btn as HTMLButtonElement).textContent?.includes(text) ?? false,
  );
}

describe('WwellIrEditorComponent', () => {
  let fixture: ComponentFixture<WwellIrEditorComponent>;

  async function setup(overrides: Partial<IntegratedRequirement> = {}): Promise<void> {
    await TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      imports: [WwellIrEditorComponent, NoopAnimationsModule],
      providers: [
        { provide: WwellIrStore, useValue: storeMock },
        { provide: NotificationService, useValue: notificationServiceMock },
        { provide: S3Service, useValue: s3ServiceMock },
      ],
    }).compileComponents();

    fixture = TestBed.createComponent(WwellIrEditorComponent);
    fixture.componentRef.setInput('ir', buildIr(overrides));
    fixture.detectChanges();
    // The editor's `viewMode` model defaults to Timeline (see its own "defaults to Timeline view on
    // load" test below) — every other test here exercises section read/edit logic that has
    // nothing to do with which view mode is active, so they're pinned to the always-everything-
    // visible Sections layout rather than depending on whatever the current default happens to be.
    // Goes through the real toggle button (setViewMode is protected) like a user would.
    const sectionsViewBtn: HTMLButtonElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Sections view"]');
    sectionsViewBtn.click();
    fixture.detectChanges();
  }

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    creatingIrSignal.set(false);
    createIrErrorSignal.set(null);
    deletingIrSignal.set(false);
    updatingIrStatusSignal.set(false);
    recallingIrSignal.set(false);
  });

  afterEach(() => fixture?.destroy());

  it('should create', async () => {
    await setup();
    expect(fixture.componentInstance).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('defaults to Timeline view on load', async () => {
    // Deliberately bypasses setup()'s own forced switch to Sections (see its comment) to observe
    // the raw post-ngOnInit state.
    await TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      imports: [WwellIrEditorComponent, NoopAnimationsModule],
      providers: [
        { provide: WwellIrStore, useValue: storeMock },
        { provide: NotificationService, useValue: notificationServiceMock },
        { provide: S3Service, useValue: s3ServiceMock },
      ],
    }).compileComponents();
    fixture = TestBed.createComponent(WwellIrEditorComponent);
    fixture.componentRef.setInput('ir', buildIr());
    fixture.detectChanges();

    const timelineViewBtn: HTMLButtonElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Timeline view"]');
    expect(timelineViewBtn.classList).toContain('ire__view-toggle-btn--active');
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.ire__rail')).toBeNull();
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.ire__tl-row')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('renders one IR SECTIONS rail group per top-level entry, and one section anchor per leaf', async () => {
    await setup();
    const groups = fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('.ire__rail-group');
    const sections = fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('[id^="ire-sec-"]');
    expect(groups.length).toBe(WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL.length);
    expect(sections.length).toBe(wwellIrRailLeaves(WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL).length);
  });

  it('opens Water Well Details straight into edit mode for a not-yet-created IR', async () => {
    await setup({ irSeq: null });
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.ire__title').textContent).toContain('HAIL-500');
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('app-dynamic-section')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Edit Water Well Details"]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('saving Water Well Details persists via store.createIr and closes edit mode', async () => {
    await setup({ irSeq: null });

    findButtonByText(fixture, 'Save')?.click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    expect(storeMock.createIr).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('app-dynamic-section')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('never renders the Water Well Details pencil once the IR has actually been created (has a real irSeq)', async () => {
    await setup({ irSeq: 9001 });
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Edit Water Well Details"]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('adding a casing row grows the section FormArray while Casing is being edited', async () => {
    await setup();
    const editBtn: HTMLButtonElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Edit Casing"]');
    editBtn.click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    findButtonByText(fixture, 'Add Casing')?.click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    const rows = fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('.rpg__row');
    expect(rows.length).toBe(1);
  });

  it('shows Submit for Review while the record is still draft', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'draft' });
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('hides Submit for Review while no Location Map is attached', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'draft', attachments: [] });
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('hides Submit for Review while the WLDA attachment is missing', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'draft', attachments: buildIr().attachments.filter(attachment => attachment.slot !== 'wlda') });
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('hides Submit for Review once the record is approved', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'approved' });
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  /** A page reload can't tell a genuinely untouched 'in-progress' record from one already
   *  submitted to SAP (the list endpoint has no sap_wf_id column — see
   *  wwell-ir-summary.mapper.ts — so every non-approved row defaults to 'in-progress' regardless
   *  of history). Once a detail fetch resolves a real sapWfId, that must gate the button even
   *  though `status` alone would still say it's submittable. */
  it('hides Submit for Review once the record already has a real sapWfId, even while status still reads in-progress', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'in-progress', sapWfId: 11033 });
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  // The refresh button sits outside the header pill's popover (next to the pill itself, so
  // refreshing never requires opening it) — a plain part of this fixture's own template, not
  // portaled by CDK the way the popover's own content is, so no need to click the pill open first.
  it('hides the approval refresh button for a record never submitted (no sapWfId)', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'draft', sapWfId: null });
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Refresh approval status"]')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('the approval refresh button calls store.refreshIrApprovalStatus for the open record', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'submit-for-review', irSeq: 9001, sapWfId: 11033 });

    const refreshBtn: HTMLButtonElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Refresh approval status"]');
    expect(refreshBtn).toBeTruthy();
    refreshBtn.click();

    expect(storeMock.refreshIrApprovalStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(9001, 11033);
  });

  it('Submit for Review calls store.submitIrForReview directly (no re-save first)', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'in-progress' });
    findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')?.click();

    expect(storeMock.createIr).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(storeMock.submitIrForReview).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('shows Submit for Review again, not Recall, once the workflow comes back Recalled', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'submit-for-review', sapWfId: 6, sapWfStatusText: 'Recalled' });

    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Recall')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('shows Recall, not Submit for Review, while the workflow is still in progress', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'submit-for-review', sapWfId: 6, sapWfStatusText: 'In Progress' });

    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Recall')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('shows Submit for Review again, not Recall, once the workflow was Rejected', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'submit-for-review', sapWfId: 6, sapWfStatusText: 'Rejected' });

    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Recall')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('shows neither action once the workflow is Approved', async () => {
    await setup({ status: 'submit-for-review', sapWfId: 6, sapWfStatusText: 'Approved' });

    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Submit for Review')).toBeFalsy();
    expect(findFooterButtonByText(fixture, 'Recall')).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('editing a section on an already-submitted record saves it back into in-progress', async () => {
    // Water Well Details is permanently locked once an IR is created (has a real irSeq — see the
    // "never renders the Water Well Details pencil" test above), so this exercises the same
    // save-reverts-to-in-progress behavior through Casing instead.
    await setup({ status: 'approved' });
    const editBtn: HTMLButtonElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('[aria-label="Edit Casing"]');
    editBtn.click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    findButtonByText(fixture, 'Save')?.click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    const [payload] = storeMock.createIr.mock.calls[0];
    expect(payload.status).toBe('in-progress');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { WwellIrTimelineStepStatus, wwellIrTimelineStepStatus } from './wwell-ir-timeline.util';

export type WwellIrEditorViewMode = 'sections' | 'timeline';

const SELECT_PANEL_CLASS = 'ire-select-panel';

function findSection(schema: ReturnType<typeof buildWwellIrFormSchema>, stepKey: string, sectionKey: string): WwellFormSectionConfig {
  const section = schema.find(step => step.key === stepKey)?.sections.find(s => s.key === sectionKey);
  if (!section) throw new Error(`Missing IR form section ${stepKey}.${sectionKey}`);
  return { ...section, title: null };
}

/** A repeatable config for the section's "+" add-form — same row shape as the full section's own
 *  repeatable config, `requiredKeys` (the fields Save identifies a row by, so a row can't be added
 *  without them) additionally marked required. Rendered through the same `<app-repeatable-group>`
 *  the full section's own edit view uses, so each staged row gets its own remove (✕) button for
 *  free — but `showAddButton: false` hides that component's own "+ Add Row" button, since the
 *  section-head "+" icon already appends a row on every click (`addStagedRow`); keeping both would
 *  be two buttons doing the same thing. `minRows` is deliberately omitted (defaults to 0) — the
 *  array starts empty and `addStagedRow` adds exactly one row per click, including the first,
 *  rather than the click handler and a `minRows: 1` seed both trying to supply that first row. */
function addRowsSection(section: WwellFormSectionConfig, requiredKeys: readonly string[], addLabel: string): WwellFormSectionConfig {
  const rowFields = section.repeatable?.rowFields ?? [];
  const key = `${section.key}NewRows`;
  return {
    key,
    title: null,
    repeatable: {
      key,
      addLabel,
      showAddButton: false,
      rowFields: rowFields.map(field =>
        requiredKeys.includes(field.key) ? { ...field, validators: { ...field.validators, required: true } } : field,
      ),
    },
  };
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir-editor',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    NgTemplateOutlet,
    OverlayModule,

    MatProgressBarModule,
```
