## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-detail-response.mapper.ts

```ts
import {
  IntegratedRequirement,
  IntegratedRequirementTestType,
  IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { isLegacyIrHtmlText, stripLegacyIrHtml } from './wwell-ir-legacy-html.util';
import { parseOpenHoleSizeFromRemarks, resolveCompletionTypeFromFlags } from './wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper';
import { formatUtmCoordinates } from '../wwell-ir-coordinates.util';
import {
  IrCasingRow,
  IrHeaderRow,
  IrHydrogeologyRow,
  IrLinkedWellRow,
  IrTopsRow,
  IrWaterRow,
  IrWellDesignRow,
  WwellIrDetailTableBlock,
  WwellIrTableName,
} from './wwell-ir-payload.model';

function normalizeRowKeys<TRow>(row: Record<string, unknown>): TRow {
  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(row)) {
    normalized[key.toLowerCase()] = row[key];
  }
  return normalized as TRow;
}

function rowsFor<TRow>(blocks: readonly WwellIrDetailTableBlock[], table: WwellIrTableName): readonly TRow[] {
  const block = blocks.find(candidate => candidate.table.toLowerCase() === table);
  return (block?.data ?? []).map(row => normalizeRowKeys<TRow>(row));
}

function resolveGeologistName(
  empNum: number | null | undefined,
  geologistNameByEmpNum: Readonly<Record<number, string>>,
  fallback: string | null,
): string | null {
  if (empNum == null) return fallback;
  return geologistNameByEmpNum[empNum] ?? fallback;
}

function parseH2sPpm(h2s: string | null | undefined): number | null {
  if (!h2s) return null;
  const value = parseFloat(h2s);
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}

function normalizeFlag(value: unknown): 'Y' | 'N' {
  return value === 'Y' ? 'Y' : 'N';
}

function normalizeRemarks(value: unknown): string | null {
  return typeof value === 'string' && value ? value : null;
}

function mapWellDesignFlags(row: IrWellDesignRow | undefined): IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags | null {
  if (!row) return null;
  return {
    ohFlg: normalizeFlag(row.oh_flg),
    ohRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.oh_remarks),
    gpFlg: normalizeFlag(row.gp_flg),
    gpRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.gp_remarks),
    perforatedFlg: normalizeFlag(row.perforated_flg),
    perforatedRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.perforated_remarks),
    lsFlg: normalizeFlag(row.ls_flg),
    lsRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.ls_remarks),
  };
}

/** The row the record's own saves write to: the one it already holds (so a save UPDATEs it), else the newest
 *  — GET returns every row for the well unordered, and reading `[0]` picked an old, empty one over the
 *  completion the user had just saved, which then made each later save INSERT yet another row. */
function pickWellDesignRow(rows: readonly IrWellDesignRow[], heldWdSeq: number | null | undefined): IrWellDesignRow | undefined {
  const held = rows.find(row => row.wd_seq === heldWdSeq);
  if (held) return held;
  return rows.reduce<IrWellDesignRow | undefined>((newest, row) => (!newest || (row.wd_seq ?? 0) > (newest.wd_seq ?? 0) ? row : newest), undefined);
}

/** A remark column the backend returns as null/absent on a header row that *is* there means the text was
 *  deleted — so it reads as blank, not as "keep what the client last had" (which left a deleted
 *  Mud Logging / Sampling / Flow Test... showing until a full reload). The fallback only applies when the
 *  response carried no header row at all. */
function resolveLegacyText(raw: string | null | undefined, fallback: string, headerPresent: boolean): string {
  if (raw != null) return stripLegacyIrHtml(raw);
  return headerPresent ? '' : fallback;
}

export function mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement(
  existing: IntegratedRequirement,
  blocks: readonly WwellIrDetailTableBlock[],
  geologistNameByEmpNum: Readonly<Record<number, string>> = {},
): IntegratedRequirement {
  const header = rowsFor<IrHeaderRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_header')[0] as IrHeaderRow | undefined;
  const casingRows = rowsFor<IrCasingRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_casing');
  const topsRows = rowsFor<IrTopsRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_tops');
  const hydroRow = rowsFor<IrHydrogeologyRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_hydrogeology')[0] as IrHydrogeologyRow | undefined;
  const waterRows = rowsFor<IrWaterRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_water');
  const linkedWell = rowsFor<IrLinkedWellRow>(blocks, 'exad_rcd_prewap')[0] as IrLinkedWellRow | undefined;
  const wellDesignRow = pickWellDesignRow(rowsFor<IrWellDesignRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_well_design'), existing.wellDesignSeq);
  const headerPresent = header !== undefined;
  const completionText = resolveLegacyText(header?.completion ?? header?.cmpl_type, existing.programDetails.completion, headerPresent);
  const persistedFlags = wellDesignRow ? mapWellDesignFlags(wellDesignRow) : null;
  // exad_gwd_well_design is keyed by prewap_seq alone, so a well can hold several rows (every INSERT adds
  // one). Whatever row is picked is this record's design — including one whose flags are all 'N': that is
  // a completion that was *deleted*, and it must read back as blank, not fall back to the stale value.
  const wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr = !!wellDesignRow;
  const resolvedType = wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? resolveCompletionTypeFromFlags(persistedFlags) : null;
  const hasLegacyMarkup = [
    header?.completion ?? header?.cmpl_type,
    header?.acidization,
    header?.dt_rmks,
    header?.logging,
    header?.mud_rmks,
    header?.sampling,
    header?.flow_rmks,
    header?.dev_pump_test,
  ].some(isLegacyIrHtmlText);

  return {
    ...existing,
    hasLegacyMarkup,
    wellDesignSeq: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? wellDesignRow.wd_seq : existing.wellDesignSeq,
    sapWfId: header?.sap_wf_id ?? existing.sapWfId,
    requestReference: {
      ...existing.requestReference,
      linkedRequestId: header?.prewap_seq != null ? String(header.prewap_seq) : existing.requestReference.linkedRequestId,
      letterNumber: header?.ltr_no ?? existing.requestReference.letterNumber,
      wellName: linkedWell ? `${linkedWell.w_prim_hid ?? ''}_${linkedWell.w_num ?? ''}` : existing.requestReference.wellName,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: linkedWell?.sv_surf_elevf ?? existing.requestReference.surfaceElevationFtAsl,
      // `surfacen`/`surfacee` are UTM northing/easting meters, not decimal-degree lat/long — shown
      // as the raw survey values (Northing/Easting/Zone), not converted — see
      // wwell-ir-coordinates.util.ts's doc comment.
      coordinates:
        linkedWell?.surfacen != null && linkedWell?.surfacee != null
          ? (formatUtmCoordinates(linkedWell.surfacen, linkedWell.surfacee, linkedWell.sv_utm_zn) ??
            existing.requestReference.coordinates)
          : existing.requestReference.coordinates,
      targetDepthFt: linkedWell?.target_dpth ?? existing.requestReference.targetDepthFt,
      targetAquifer: linkedWell?.target_form ?? existing.requestReference.targetAquifer,
      supportingBusiness: linkedWell?.expl_wb_name ?? existing.requestReference.supportingBusiness,
    },
    formationTops: topsRows.map(row => ({
      name: row.st_long_cd,
      depthFt: row.plan_tvd_dpth,
      subSeaFtAsl: row.plan_ss_dpth ?? null,
    })),
    topsRemarks: header?.tops_remarks ?? existing.topsRemarks,
    contacts: {
      geologistPrimary:
        resolveGeologistName(header?.cont_geologist, geologistNameByEmpNum, existing.contacts.geologistPrimary) ??
        existing.contacts.geologistPrimary,
      geologistSecondary: resolveGeologistName(header?.sub_cont_geologist, geologistNameByEmpNum, existing.contacts.geologistSecondary),
    },
    offsetWellsRemarks: header?.water_remarks ?? existing.offsetWellsRemarks,
    offsetWells: waterRows.map(row => ({
      epANum: row.ep_a_num,
      wellName: row.w_gnr_name ?? null,
      distanceKm: row.distanct ?? null,
      location: row.direction ?? null,
      aquifer: row.aquifer ?? null,
      totalDepthFt: row.td ?? null,
      testDurationMin: row.test_duration ?? null,
      rateGpm: row.rate ?? null,
      rpm: row.rpm ?? null,
      swlFt: row.swl ?? null,
      pwlFt: row.final_pwl ?? null,
      drawdownFt: row.drawdown ?? null,
      specificCapacity: row.spec_capacity ?? null,
      tdsPpm: row.field_tds ?? null,
      h2sPpm: row.h2s ?? null,
    })),
    casing: casingRows.map(row => ({
      type: row.csg_type,
      sizeIn: row.csg_size,
      depthFtBgl: row.csg_depth,
      tolFtBgl: null,
      remarks: row.csg_remarks ?? null,
    })),
    programDetails: {
      ...existing.programDetails,
      completionType: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? (resolvedType?.completionType ?? '') : existing.programDetails.completionType,
      completion: completionText,
      linerSizeIn: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? (resolvedType?.linerSizeIn ?? null) : existing.programDetails.linerSizeIn,
      linerDepthFt: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? (resolvedType?.linerDepthFt ?? null) : existing.programDetails.linerDepthFt,
      // oh_remarks is the only backend column open hole size ever reached (see
      // deriveCompletionElementFlags/parseOpenHoleSizeFromRemarks in wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper.ts)
      // — without parsing it back out here, the Open Hole Size input silently reverted to blank
      // after every save+reload even though the value was sitting right there in oh_remarks.
      openHoleSizeIn: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr
        ? parseOpenHoleSizeFromRemarks(wellDesignRow.oh_remarks)
        : existing.programDetails.openHoleSizeIn,
      acidization: resolveLegacyText(header?.acidization, existing.programDetails.acidization, headerPresent),
      transmission: resolveLegacyText(header?.dt_rmks, existing.programDetails.transmission, headerPresent),
      developmentTesting: {
        logging: resolveLegacyText(header?.logging, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.logging, headerPresent),
        mudLogging: resolveLegacyText(header?.mud_rmks, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.mudLogging, headerPresent),
        sampling: resolveLegacyText(header?.sampling, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.sampling, headerPresent),
        flowTest: resolveLegacyText(header?.flow_rmks, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.flowTest, headerPresent),
        remarks: resolveLegacyText(header?.dev_pump_test, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.remarks, headerPresent),
      },
      wellDesignFlags: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? persistedFlags : existing.programDetails.wellDesignFlags,
    },
    hydrogeology: hydroRow
      ? {
          aquifer: hydroRow.st_long_cd,
          estimatedTdsPpm: hydroRow.est_water_quality,
          h2sPpm: parseH2sPpm(hydroRow.h2s),
          estimatedPumpDepthFt: wellDesignRow?.pump_lvl ?? existing.hydrogeology.estimatedPumpDepthFt,
        }
      : existing.hydrogeology,
    testProgram: {
      ...existing.testProgram,
      testType: (header?.test_type as IntegratedRequirementTestType | null | undefined) ?? existing.testProgram.testType,
      estimatedRateGpm: hydroRow?.est_productivity ?? existing.testProgram.estimatedRateGpm,
      estimatedWaterLevelFt: hydroRow?.est_sta_water_lvl ?? existing.testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
      siwhpPsi: hydroRow?.siwhp ?? existing.testProgram.siwhpPsi,
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import { computed, signal, type Signal } from '@angular/core';
import { WwellFieldConfig, WwellFieldOption, WwellFormStepConfig } from '../../shared/dynamic-form/wwell-dynamic-form.types';
import { WWELL_APPROVAL_WELL_NAME_PATTERN } from '../../shared/wwell-approvals/wwell-approvals.config';
import {
  WWELL_ACIDIZATION_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_COMPLETION_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_DEVELOPMENT_FLOW_TEST_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_DEVELOPMENT_TESTING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_LOGGING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_MUD_LOGGING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_SAMPLING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_TEST_TYPE_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_TRANSMISSION_OPTIONS,
} from '../../shared/wwell-planning-reference-data.config';
import { WWELL_COMPLETION_TYPE, WWELL_DEFAULT_LINER_SIZE, WWELL_LINER_COMPLETION_TYPES } from './wwell-ir-completion-type.config';
import { WWELL_IR_CASING_SIZE_MENU, WWELL_IR_CASING_TYPE_MENU, WWELL_IR_DROPDOWN_MENU_BY_FIELD } from './wwell-ir-dropdown-menu.config';
import { toUtmAxes } from '../wwell-ir-coordinates.util';

/** Splits the field's single "Northing X, Easting Y, Zone Z" value into its own Northing/
 *  Easting/UTM Zone box, shown exactly as the raw survey values — same fallback `toUtmAxes` uses
 *  elsewhere: text that doesn't read as that shape (or isn't populated yet) keeps one "Coordinates"
 *  box rather than showing empty Easting/Zone boxes next to it. */
function splitCoordinatesDisplay(value: string): readonly { readonly label: string; readonly value: string }[] {
  if (!value) return [{ label: 'Coordinates', value: '—' }];
  return toUtmAxes(value).map(axis => ({ label: axis.label, value: axis.value }));
}

        fields: [
          {
            key: 'file',
            label: 'Well Location and Drilling Approval (WLDA)',
            type: 'file',
            placeholder: 'Click to attach file...',
            validators: { required: true },
          },
        ],
      },
    ],

export function buildWwellIrFormSchema(
  linkedRequestOptions: Signal<readonly WwellFieldOption[]>,
  geologistOptions: Signal<readonly string[]>,
  dropdownMenus: Signal<Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>>> = signal({}),
  aquiferOptions: Signal<readonly WwellFieldOption[]> = signal([]),
  formationOptions: Signal<readonly WwellFieldOption[]> = signal([]),
): readonly WwellFormStepConfig[] {
  return WWELL_IR_FORM_SCHEMA.map(step => {
    if (step.key === 'subsurface') {
      return {
        ...step,
        sections: step.sections.map(section =>
          section.key === 'formationTops' && section.repeatable
            ? {
                ...section,
                repeatable: {
                  ...section.repeatable,
                  rowFields: section.repeatable.rowFields.map(field => (field.key === 'name' ? { ...field, options: formationOptions } : field)),
                },
              }
            : section.key === 'casing' && section.repeatable
              ? {
                  ...section,
                  repeatable: {
                    ...section.repeatable,
                    rowFields: section.repeatable.rowFields.map(field => {
                      if (field.key === 'type') return { ...field, options: computed(() => dropdownMenus()[WWELL_IR_CASING_TYPE_MENU] ?? []) };
                      if (field.key === 'sizeIn') return { ...field, options: computed(() => dropdownMenus()[WWELL_IR_CASING_SIZE_MENU] ?? []) };
                      return field;
                    }),
                  },
                }
              : section,
        ),
      };
    }
    if (step.key === 'hydrogeology') {
      return {
        ...step,
        sections: step.sections.map(section => ({
          ...section,
          fields: section.fields?.map(field => (field.key === 'aquifer' ? { ...field, options: aquiferOptions } : field)),
        })),
      };
    }
    if (step.key === 'programDetails') {
      return {
        ...step,
        sections: step.sections.map(section => ({
          ...section,
          fields: section.fields?.map(field => withDropdownMenuOptions(field, dropdownMenus)),
        })),
      };
    }
    if (step.key === 'reference') {
      return {
        ...step,
        sections: step.sections.map(section => {
          if (section.key !== 'main' || !section.fields) return section;
          return {
            ...section,
            fields: section.fields.map(field =>
              field.key === 'linkedRequestId' ? { ...field, options: linkedRequestOptions } : field,
            ),
          };
        }),
      };
    }
    if (step.key === 'contacts') {
      return {
        ...step,
        sections: step.sections.map(section => {
          if (section.key !== 'geologist' || !section.fields) return section;
          return {
            ...section,
            fields: section.fields.map(field =>
              field.key === 'geologistPrimary' || field.key === 'geologistSecondary' ? { ...field, options: geologistOptions } : field,
            ),
          };
        }),
      };
    }
    return step;
  });
}

/** Drives a Program Details dropdown purely from its EXAD_DHUB_LKUP_DATA menu — empty until loaded,
 *  and empty if the table has no rows for it (any fallback lives in WwellIrStore.loadDropdownMenus). */
function withDropdownMenuOptions(field: WwellFieldConfig, dropdownMenus: Signal<Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>>>): WwellFieldConfig {
  const menu = WWELL_IR_DROPDOWN_MENU_BY_FIELD[field.key];
  if (!menu) return field;
  return { ...field, options: computed(() => dropdownMenus()[menu] ?? []) };
}
```

```ts
import { ApplicationRef, EnvironmentInjector, Injectable, createComponent, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { firstValueFrom } from 'rxjs';
import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { IrAttachment } from '@models/wwell-planning/ir-attachment.model';
import { S3Service } from '@services/s3/s3.service';
import { WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_SLOTS, irAttachmentsFor } from './wwell-ir-attachments.config';
import { appendAttachmentsToLetter, IrLetterAttachmentFile } from './wwell-ir-letter-attachments.util';
import { hasWellboreDiagramDataForIntegratedRequirement } from './wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper';
import { resolveLetterStampStatus, resolveLetterWorkflow } from './wwell-ir.config';
import { WwellIrService } from './wwell-ir.service';
import { WwellIrViewLetterComponent } from './wwell-ir-view/wwell-ir-view-letter/wwell-ir-view-letter.component';

/** The wellbore diagram's D3 entrance animation (see ANIM in wellbore-diagram.model.ts — staggered
 *  casing/open-hole/gravel reveals) must finish before the SVG is rasterized, or the snapshot
 *  catches a half-drawn well. Rather than a fixed sleep, the capture waits until the off-screen
 *  copy has stopped changing (`DIAGRAM_QUIET_MS` without a DOM mutation), bounded below and above
 *  so a diagram that never mutates still captures and one that never stops still does. */
const DIAGRAM_MIN_WAIT_MS = 500;
const DIAGRAM_QUIET_MS = 600;
const DIAGRAM_MAX_WAIT_MS = 4000;
const OFFSCREEN_LETTER_WIDTH_PX = 900;
const DIAGRAM_CAPTURE_ATTEMPTS = 3;

/** Resolves once `host` has gone `DIAGRAM_QUIET_MS` without a DOM mutation (never sooner than
 *  `DIAGRAM_MIN_WAIT_MS`, never later than `DIAGRAM_MAX_WAIT_MS`). D3 transitions write attributes
 *  every frame, so silence means the entrance animation is finished. */
export function waitForDiagramToSettle(host: HTMLElement): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    let quietTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => armQuietTimer());
    const finish = () => {
      observer.disconnect();
      clearTimeout(quietTimer);
      clearTimeout(maxTimer);
      resolve();
    };
    function armQuietTimer(): void {
      clearTimeout(quietTimer);
      quietTimer = setTimeout(finish, DIAGRAM_QUIET_MS);
    }
    const maxTimer = setTimeout(finish, DIAGRAM_MAX_WAIT_MS);
    observer.observe(host, { subtree: true, childList: true, attributes: true, characterData: true });
    quietTimer = setTimeout(finish, Math.max(DIAGRAM_MIN_WAIT_MS, DIAGRAM_QUIET_MS));
  });
}

/** Reads a `Blob` via `FileReader` rather than `Blob.arrayBuffer()` — the latter isn't implemented
 *  by jsdom (this repo's test environment), while `FileReader.readAsArrayBuffer` is. */
export function blobToArrayBuffer(blob: Blob): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result as ArrayBuffer);
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(blob);
  });
}

/**
 * Builds the complete IR letter PDF — the same document the view's "Download PDF" produces: the
 * backend-rendered letter (with the wellbore diagram, stamp and workflow section) followed by every
 * stored attachment appended as trailing pages. Shared by the download and by Submit for Review, so
 * what SAP receives is always byte-for-byte what a user would download for the same record.
 */
@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class WwellIrLetterPdfService {
  private readonly irService = inject(WwellIrService);
  private readonly s3 = inject(S3Service);
  private readonly appRef = inject(ApplicationRef);
  private readonly environmentInjector = inject(EnvironmentInjector);

  /** Renders the letter, with the diagram captured from an off-screen copy of the Letter tab —
   *  needed when no Letter tab is on screen (e.g. submitting from the editor). Never yields a
   *  letter missing its diagram: if the record has diagram data and the capture keeps failing, this
   *  rejects instead. Also rejects if the backend refuses to render the letter (the caller owns the
   *  error message). */
  async buildLetterPdf(ir: IntegratedRequirement, omitApprovalList = false): Promise<Blob> {
    if (ir.irSeq == null) throw new Error('Save the integrated requirement before generating its letter.');
    const wellboreImageBase64 = await this.captureRequiredWellboreImage(ir);
    const letter = await firstValueFrom(
      this.irService.downloadLetterPdf(ir.irSeq, wellboreImageBase64, resolveLetterStampStatus(ir), resolveLetterWorkflow(ir) ?? undefined, omitApprovalList),
    );
    return this.mergeAttachments(letter, ir);
  }

  /** Merges the IR's stored attachments onto the rendered letter entirely client-side. Each
   *  attachment is fetched independently so one unreadable file is dropped without losing the
   *  others, and any failure of the merge itself falls back to the unmerged letter — a broken
   *  appendix should never turn a working letter into a failed one. */
  async mergeAttachments(letterBlob: Blob, ir: IntegratedRequirement): Promise<Blob> {
    if (ir.attachments.length === 0) return letterBlob;

    try {
      const ordered: IrAttachment[] = WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_SLOTS.flatMap(config => irAttachmentsFor(ir.attachments, config.slot));
      const fetched = await Promise.all(ordered.map(attachment => this.fetchAttachmentFile(attachment)));
      const files = fetched.filter((file): file is IrLetterAttachmentFile => file !== null);
      const merged = await appendAttachmentsToLetter(await blobToArrayBuffer(letterBlob), files);
      return new Blob([new Uint8Array(merged)], { type: 'application/pdf' });
    } catch {
      return letterBlob;
    }
  }

  private async fetchAttachmentFile(attachment: IrAttachment): Promise<IrLetterAttachmentFile | null> {
    try {
      const file = await firstValueFrom(this.s3.download(attachment.objectKey, attachment.fileName));
      return { label: attachment.label, contentType: attachment.contentType, bytes: await blobToArrayBuffer(file) };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private async captureRequiredWellboreImage(ir: IntegratedRequirement): Promise<string | undefined> {
    if (!hasWellboreDiagramDataForIntegratedRequirement(ir)) return undefined;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < DIAGRAM_CAPTURE_ATTEMPTS; attempt++) {
      const image = await this.captureWellboreImageOffscreen(ir);
      if (image) return image;
    }
    throw new Error('Could not render the wellbore diagram for the IR letter. Please try submitting again.');
  }

  /** Mounts the real Letter component off-screen (laid out, so the diagram gets real dimensions),
   *  waits out the diagram animation, captures it, then tears everything down. Yields `undefined`
   *  when the capture fails; the caller decides whether that is acceptable. */
  private async captureWellboreImageOffscreen(ir: IntegratedRequirement): Promise<string | undefined> {
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    host.style.cssText = `position:fixed;top:0;left:-10000px;width:${OFFSCREEN_LETTER_WIDTH_PX}px;pointer-events:none;`;
    document.body.appendChild(host);
    const componentRef = createComponent(WwellIrViewLetterComponent, { environmentInjector: this.environmentInjector, hostElement: host });
    try {
      componentRef.setInput('ir', ir);
      this.appRef.attachView(componentRef.hostView);
      componentRef.changeDetectorRef.detectChanges();
      await waitForDiagramToSettle(host);
      return await componentRef.instance.captureWellboreImage();
    } catch {
      return undefined;
    } finally {
      this.appRef.detachView(componentRef.hostView);
      componentRef.destroy();
      host.remove();
    }
  }
}
```

```html
<article class="letter" [class.wp-paper]="printing()">
  <header class="letter__masthead">
    <div>
      <p class="letter__org">Groundwater Division</p>
      <h3 class="letter__doc-title">Integrated Requirements Letter</h3>
    </div>
    <dl class="letter__meta">
      <div>
        <dt>Date</dt>
        <dd>{{ issueDate }}</dd>
      </div>
      <div>
        <dt>IR #</dt>
        <dd>{{ ir().code }}</dd>
      </div>
      @if (ir().requestReference.letterNumber; as letterNumber) {
        <div>
          <dt>Letter #</dt>
          <dd>{{ letterNumber }}</dd>
        </div>
      }
    </dl>
  </header>

  <p class="letter__subject">
    <strong>Subject:</strong> Integrated Requirements for Water Well {{ ir().requestReference.wellName || '—' }}
  </p>

  <p class="letter__intro">
    Following are the integrated requirements for the drilling water supply well
    <strong>{{ ir().requestReference.wellName || '—' }}</strong>.
  </p>

  @if (ir().requestReference.supportingBusiness; as supporting) {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Objectives</h4>
      <p class="letter__text">
        <strong>{{ ir().requestReference.wellName || '—' }}</strong> will be used as drilling water supply well supporting
        Development well <strong>{{ supporting }}</strong>.
      </p>
    </section>
  }

  <!-- Section order mirrors the downloaded PDF (agwa-planning's irLetter.html). -->
  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Well Location</h4>
    <div class="letter__table-wrap">
      <table class="letter__table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Easting</th>
            <th>Northing</th>
            <th>UTM Zone</th>
            <th>Elevation (GL.) Est.</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr>
            <td>{{ axisValue('Easting') }}</td>
            <td>{{ axisValue('Northing') }}</td>
            <td>{{ axisValue('UTM Zone') }}</td>
            <td>{{ elevationLabel() }}</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Casing Program</h4>
    <div class="letter__table-wrap">
      <table class="letter__table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Type</th>
            <th>Casing/Liner Size</th>
            <th>Depth (ft bgl)</th>
            <th>Remarks</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          @for (row of ir().casing; track row.type + row.depthFtBgl) {
            <tr>
              <td>{{ row.type }}</td>
              <td>{{ row.sizeIn }}</td>
              <td>{{ row.depthFtBgl }}</td>
              <td>{{ row.remarks || '' }}</td>
            </tr>
          } @empty {
            <tr><td colspan="4" class="letter__table-empty">No casing program recorded.</td></tr>
          }
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </section>

  @if (completionDesign(); as design) {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Completion</h4>
      <p class="letter__text">{{ design }}</p>
    </section>
  } @else {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Completion</h4>
      <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">Not provided</p>
    </section>
  }

  @if (ir().programDetails.developmentTesting.logging; as logging) {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Logging</h4>
      <p class="letter__text">{{ logging }}</p>
    </section>
  } @else {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Logging</h4>
      <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">Not provided</p>
    </section>
  }

  @if (ir().programDetails.acidization; as acidization) {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Acidization</h4>
      <p class="letter__text">{{ acidization }}</p>
    </section>
  } @else {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Acidization</h4>
      <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">Not provided</p>
    </section>
  }

  @if (ir().programDetails.transmission || ir().programDetails.developmentTesting.mudLogging) {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Mud Logging &amp; Data Transmission</h4>
      @if (ir().programDetails.transmission; as transmission) {
        <p class="letter__text">{{ transmission }}</p>
      }
      @if (ir().programDetails.developmentTesting.mudLogging; as mudLogging) {
        <p class="letter__text">{{ mudLogging }}</p>
      }
    </section>
  } @else {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Mud Logging &amp; Data Transmission</h4>
      <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">Not provided</p>
    </section>
  }

  @if (ir().programDetails.developmentTesting.sampling; as sampling) {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Sampling</h4>
      <p class="letter__text">{{ sampling }}</p>
    </section>
  } @else {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Sampling</h4>
      <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">Not provided</p>
    </section>
  }

  <!-- Hydrology Test Type picks one: a flow test prints Flow Test only, a pump test Development Pump Test only. -->
  @if (isFlowTest()) {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Flow Test</h4>
      @if (ir().programDetails.developmentTesting.flowTest; as flowTest) {
        <p class="letter__text">{{ flowTest }}</p>
      } @else {
        <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">Not provided</p>
      }
    </section>
  } @else {
    <section class="letter__section">
      <h4>Development Pump Test</h4>
      @if (ir().programDetails.developmentTesting.remarks; as devPumpTest) {
        <p class="letter__text">{{ devPumpTest }}</p>
      } @else {
        <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">Not provided</p>
      }
    </section>
  }

  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Formation Tops*</h4>
    <div class="letter__table-wrap">
      <table class="letter__table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Horizon</th>
            <th>Depth (ft bgl)</th>
            <th>Subsea Depth (ft asl)</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          @for (top of topsRows(); track top.name) {
            <tr>
              <td>{{ top.name }}</td>
              <td>{{ top.depthFt }}</td>
              <td>{{ top.subSeaFt ?? '' }}</td>
            </tr>
          } @empty {
            <tr><td colspan="3" class="letter__table-empty">No formation tops recorded.</td></tr>
          }
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    @if (ir().topsRemarks; as topsRemarks) {
      <p class="letter__text">*{{ topsRemarks }}</p>
    }
  </section>

  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Hydrogeology</h4>
    <dl class="letter__kv">
      <div><dt>Aquifer</dt><dd>{{ ir().hydrogeology.aquifer || 'N/A' }}</dd></div>
      @if (isFlowTest()) {
        @if (ir().testProgram.siwhpPsi !== null && ir().testProgram.siwhpPsi !== undefined) {
          <div><dt>SIWHP(psi)</dt><dd>{{ ir().testProgram.siwhpPsi }}</dd></div>
        }
      } @else if (ir().testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt !== null && ir().testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt !== undefined) {
        <div><dt>Estimated Water Level (ft bgl)</dt><dd>{{ ir().testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt }}</dd></div>
      }
      <div><dt>Estimated Water Quality (ppm)</dt><dd>{{ ir().hydrogeology.estimatedTdsPpm ?? 'N/A' }}</dd></div>
      <div><dt>Estimated Productivity (gpm)</dt><dd>{{ ir().testProgram.estimatedRateGpm ?? 'N/A' }}</dd></div>
      <div><dt>H2S Presence</dt><dd>{{ h2sPresence() }}</dd></div>
    </dl>
  </section>

  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Area Water Well</h4>
    @if (ir().offsetWellsRemarks; as waterRemarks) {
      <p class="letter__text">{{ waterRemarks }}</p>
    } @else {
      <p class="letter__text letter__text--empty">No remarks.</p>
    }
    <div class="letter__table-wrap">
      <table class="letter__table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Well</th>
            <th>Distance (KM)</th>
            <th>Location</th>
            <th>Aquifer</th>
            <th>Q Rate (gpm)</th>
            <th>SWL (ft bgl)</th>
            <th>Draw D. (ft)</th>
            <th>Specific Capacity (gpm/ft)</th>
            <th>TDS (mg/l)</th>
            <th>H2S</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          @for (well of ir().offsetWells; track well.epANum) {
            <tr>
              <td>{{ well.wellName || 'EP-' + well.epANum }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.distanceKm ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.location ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.aquifer ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.rateGpm ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.swlFt ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.drawdownFt ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.specificCapacity ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.tdsPpm ?? '' }}</td>
              <td>{{ well.h2sPpm ?? '' }}</td>
            </tr>
          } @empty {
            <tr><td colspan="10" class="letter__table-empty">No offset water wells recorded.</td></tr>
          }
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Contact</h4>
    <p class="letter__text">Geologist: <strong>{{ ir().contacts.geologistPrimary || 'N/A' }}</strong></p>
    @if (ir().contacts.geologistSecondary; as secondary) {
      <p class="letter__text">
        Please provide Groundwater Division with copies of the drilling program for this well as soon as possible.
        If you have any questions, call <strong>{{ secondary }}</strong>.
      </p>
    }
  </section>

  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Integrated Requirements Approval List</h4>
    <div class="letter__table-wrap">
      <table class="letter__table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Name</th>
            <th>Title</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>Approved Date</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          @for (step of ir().chain; track step.role + step.name) {
            <tr>
              <td>{{ step.name }}</td>
              <td>{{ step.role }}</td>
              <td class="letter__status" [attr.data-tone]="step.status">{{ stepStatusLabel(step.status) }}</td>
              <td>{{ step.date || 'N/A' }}</td>
            </tr>
          } @empty {
            <tr>
              <td colspan="4" class="letter__table-empty">Pending approval</td>
            </tr>
          }
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    @if (initiatorInitials(); as initials) {
      <p class="letter__text">/{{ initials }}</p>
    }
  </section>

  <section class="letter__section">
    <h4>Proposed Water Well Design</h4>
    @if (hasWellboreData()) {
      <div class="letter__diagram-row" #wellboreDiagramRow [class.letter__diagram-row--no-depth]="!hasTargetDepth()">
        <app-wwell-ir-depth-scale
          [totalDepth]="scaleDepth()"
          [targetDepth]="wellboreDiagramData().targetDepth"
          [animTrigger]="0"
          [diagramLayout]="diagramLayout"
        />
        <app-well-bore-view class="letter__diagram" [diagramData]="wellboreDiagramData()" [animTrigger]="0" [diagramLayout]="diagramLayout" />
      </div>
    } @else {
      <p class="letter__table-empty">No casing or formation top data recorded for this well.</p>
    }
  </section>

  <!-- Mirrors what the downloaded PDF actually contains: each attached document is appended as its
       own trailing page (agwa-planning's IrAttachmentPdfAppender), in this same slot order. -->
  <section class="letter__attachments">
    <h4>Attachments</h4>
    @if (ir().attachments.length) {
      <ul class="letter__attachments-list">
        @for (slot of attachmentSlots; track slot.slot) {
          @for (attachment of attachmentsFor(slot.slot); track attachment.objectKey) {
            <li>
              @if (thumbnails()[attachment.objectKey]; as thumb) {
                <img class="letter__attachment-thumb" [src]="thumb" [alt]="attachment.fileName" />
              } @else {
                <mat-icon>{{ slot.icon }}</mat-icon>
              }
              <span>{{ slot.label }} — {{ attachment.fileName }}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                mat-icon-button
                class="letter__attachment-view"
                [attr.aria-label]="'View ' + attachment.fileName"
                matTooltip="View"
                matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"
                [disabled]="openingKey() === attachment.objectKey"
                (click)="viewAttachment(attachment, slot.label)"
              >
                <mat-icon>visibility</mat-icon>
              </button>
            </li>
          }
        }
      </ul>
    } @else {
      <p class="letter__attachments-empty">No attachments uploaded.</p>
    }
  </section>
</article>
```
