## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-facts.util.ts

```ts
import { ICasingIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/casing-ir.model';
import { ITopsIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/tops-ir.model';
import { parseInchSize } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';

/** Everything the wellbore panel prints around the diagram, derived from the same data the diagram
 *  draws — nothing is estimated or filled in, so a figure appears only when its inputs exist. */
export interface WellboreFacts {
  readonly stats: readonly WellboreStat[];
  readonly casingRows: readonly WellboreCasingRow[];
  readonly topRows: readonly WellboreTopRow[];
}

export interface WellboreStat {
  readonly label: string;
  /** What the inline previews print instead: the same figure in the shorthand a well file uses. */
  readonly short: string;
  readonly value: string;
  /** A qualifier under the value, e.g. which string a shoe belongs to. */
  readonly hint?: string;
  readonly warn?: boolean;
  /** The pictogram the gauge marks its depth with. */
  readonly glyph: WellboreGlyphKind;
  /** How far down the well it is, surface to total depth. Absent until a total depth exists. */
  readonly gauge?: WellboreGauge;
}

export type WellboreGlyphKind = 'water' | 'pump' | 'shoe' | 'depth';

/** A stretch of the well drawn as a filled line: a depth is "how much below ground" (0 → the depth), the water
 *  column is where the water starts → total depth. The glyph sits on the line at `markPct`. */
export interface WellboreGauge {
  readonly fromPct: number;
  readonly toPct: number;
  readonly markPct: number;
}

export interface WellboreCasingRow {
  readonly size: string;
  readonly type: string;
  readonly depth: string;
  /** Set below the target depth: the diagram runs off its scale, and the row says why. */
  readonly belowTarget: boolean;
}

export interface WellboreTopRow {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly depth: string;
  /** Feet down to the next top (the last one runs to total depth); "—" when either end is unknown. */
  readonly interval: string;
}

export interface WellboreFactsInput {
  /** 0 when no target depth has been set — the depth-dependent figures are then left out. */
  readonly totalDepth: number;
  readonly staticWaterLevel: number;
  readonly pumpSetting: number;
  readonly casings: readonly ICasingIR[];
  readonly tops: readonly ITopsIR[];
}

const INCH_MARK = '″';
const UNIT_MARKED = /["″”]|\bin\b/i;

export const formatFeet = (value: number): string => `${value.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft`;

/** "500–3,200 ft": the unit once, at the end. */
export const formatRange = (from: number, to: number): string => `${from.toLocaleString('en-US')}–${formatFeet(to)}`;

/** "18" → 18″, "9 5/8" → 9 5/8″; a size that already carries a unit mark is left alone. */
export function formatInches(size: string): string {
  const trimmed = size.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return '';
  return UNIT_MARKED.test(trimmed) ? trimmed : `${trimmed}${INCH_MARK}`;
}

export function buildWellboreFacts(input: WellboreFactsInput): WellboreFacts {
  const total = Math.max(input.totalDepth, 0);
  const casings = sortCasingsByDepth(input.casings);

  return {
    stats: buildStats(input, total),
    casingRows: casings.map(casing => ({
      size: formatInches(casing.csgSize),
      type: casing.csgType,
      depth: formatFeet(casing.csgDepth),
      belowTarget: total > 0 && casing.csgDepth > total,
    })),
    topRows: buildTopRows(input.tops, total),
  };
}

/** Shallow to deep — the order a casing program is read in. A string with no real depth is not a
 *  program entry, so it is left out; equal depths list the wider string first. */
export function sortCasingsByDepth(casings: readonly ICasingIR[]): ICasingIR[] {
  return casings
    .filter(casing => casing.csgDepth > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a.csgDepth - b.csgDepth || (parseInchSize(b.csgSize) ?? 0) - (parseInchSize(a.csgSize) ?? 0));
}

/** A depth as a share of total depth, 0–100 (clamped: a shoe below TD sits at the end). */
export const percentOfTotal = (ft: number, total: number): number => Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (ft / total) * 100));

function buildStats(input: WellboreFactsInput, total: number): WellboreStat[] {
  const { staticWaterLevel: water, pumpSetting: pump } = input;
  // "How much below ground": filled from the surface down to the depth, the glyph at its lower end.
  const below = (ft: number): WellboreGauge | undefined => {
    if (total <= 0) return undefined;
    const pct = percentOfTotal(ft, total);
    return { fromPct: 0, toPct: pct, markPct: pct };
  };

  const stats: WellboreStat[] = [];
  if (total > 0) stats.push({ label: 'Total depth', short: 'TD', value: formatFeet(total), glyph: 'depth', gauge: below(total) });
  if (water > 0) stats.push({ label: 'Static water level', short: 'Static WL', value: formatFeet(water), glyph: 'water', gauge: below(water) });
  if (pump > 0) stats.push({ label: 'Pump setting', short: 'Pump', value: formatFeet(pump), glyph: 'pump', gauge: below(pump) });
  return stats;
}

interface TopPoint {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly depth: number;
}

/** The tops that have a code, shallow to deep. A top with no depth yet has no place in the well, so it stays out. */
function sortedTops(tops: readonly ITopsIR[]): TopPoint[] {
  return tops
    .filter(top => top.stLongCd.trim() !== '' && top.planTvdDepth > 0)
    .map(top => ({ code: top.stLongCd.trim(), depth: top.planTvdDepth }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.depth - b.depth);
}

function buildTopRows(tops: readonly ITopsIR[], total: number): WellboreTopRow[] {
  const placed = sortedTops(tops);
  const rows = tops
    .filter(top => top.stLongCd.trim() !== '')
    .sort((a, b) => a.planTvdDepth - b.planTvdDepth);

  return rows.map(top => {
    const depth = top.planTvdDepth;
    const next = placed.find(other => other.depth > depth)?.depth ?? (total > depth ? total : 0);
    return {
      code: top.stLongCd.trim(),
      depth: depth > 0 ? formatFeet(depth) : '—',
      interval: depth > 0 && next > depth ? (next - depth).toLocaleString('en-US') : '—',
    };
  });
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component.html

```html
<section
  class="wbp"
  [class.wbp--expanded]="isFilled()"
  [class.wbp--fit]="fit()"
  [class.wbp--compact]="compact()"
  [attr.aria-label]="title()"
>
  <header class="wbp__head">
    <div class="wbp__heading">
      <!-- The well's own name is the heading (the card is named for assistive tech by the section's aria-label);
           `title()` only stands in for a record with no well name yet. -->
      <h2 class="wbp__title">{{ diagramData().wellName || title() }}</h2>
      <!-- The dialog names the record under its well; the other forms sit on a page that already does, and print the
           unit line instead. -->
      @if (!expanded()) {
        <p class="wbp__sub">Depths in ft below ground level</p>
      } @else if (irCode()) {
        <p class="wbp__sub"><span class="wbp__sub-code">{{ irCode() }}</span></p>
      }
    </div>
    <!-- The IR's test type, as the one word it is — PUMP or FLOW — with its icon, when the record has one. -->
    @if (expanded() && testMode(); as mode) {
      <span
        class="wbp__mode wbp__mode--{{ mode.kind }}"
        role="img"
        [attr.aria-label]="mode.aria"
        [matTooltip]="mode.tip"
        matTooltipClass="wp-tooltip"
      >
        @if (mode.icon) {
          <mat-icon aria-hidden="true">{{ mode.icon }}</mat-icon>
        } @else {
          <app-wwell-ir-wellbore-glyph kind="pump" />
        }
        <span aria-hidden="true">{{ mode.word }}</span>
      </span>
    }
    @if (hasData()) {
      @if (expanded()) {
        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="wbp__action" aria-label="Close expanded diagram" (click)="closed.emit()">
          <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
        </button>
      } @else {
        <button
          type="button"
          mat-icon-button
          class="wbp__action"
          aria-label="Expand wellbore diagram"
          [matTooltip]="compact() ? 'Expand for full labels' : 'Expand'"
          matTooltipClass="wp-tooltip"
          (click)="expand()"
        >
          <mat-icon>open_in_full</mat-icon>
        </button>
      }
    }
  </header>

  @if (hasData()) {
    @let f = facts();

    <div class="wbp__stage">
      <div
        class="wbp__figure"
        [class.wbp__figure--no-depth]="!hasTargetDepth()"
        [style.--wbp-ratio]="ratio()"
        [style.--wbp-max-width]="maxWidthPx() + 'px'"
        [style.--wbp-max-height]="maxHeightPx() + 'px'"
      >
        @if (!isExpandedOpen()) {
          <app-wwell-ir-depth-scale
            [style.flex-basis.%]="scalePercent()"
            [totalDepth]="scaleDepth()"
            [targetDepth]="diagramData().targetDepth"
            [animTrigger]="0"
            [diagramLayout]="layout()"
          />
          <div class="wbp__plot">
            <svg class="wbp__grid" aria-hidden="true" preserveAspectRatio="none" [attr.viewBox]="gridViewBox()">
              @for (line of gridLines(); track line.depth) {
                <line x1="0" [attr.x2]="layout().wellboreViewWidth" [attr.y1]="line.y" [attr.y2]="line.y" />
              }
              @let ground = groundLine();
              <line class="wbp__ground-line" [attr.x1]="ground.x1" [attr.x2]="ground.x2" [attr.y1]="ground.y" [attr.y2]="ground.y" />
            </svg>
            <app-well-bore-view
              class="wbp__diagram"
              [diagramData]="diagramData()"
              [animTrigger]="0"
              [diagramLayout]="layout()"
              [isFullscreen]="isFilled()"
            />
          </div>
        }
      </div>
    </div>

    <section class="wbp__block wbp__block--legend" aria-label="Diagram legend">
      <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Legend</h3>
      <ul class="wbp__legend">
        @for (item of legend(); track item.key) {
          <li class="wbp__key">
            <span class="wbp__swatch wbp__swatch--{{ item.key }}" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            {{ item.label }}
          </li>
        }
      </ul>
    </section>

    <!-- The figures behind the drawing: key depths, the casing program, the formation tops — one card each,
         side by side where there is room. -->
    <div class="wbp__data">
      @if (f.stats.length > 0) {
        <section class="wbp__block wbp__block--stats" aria-label="Key depths">
          <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Key depths</h3>
          <dl class="wbp__stats">
            @for (stat of f.stats; track stat.label) {
              <ng-container *ngTemplateOutlet="statTile; context: { $implicit: stat, i: $index }" />
            }
          </dl>

        </section>
      }

      @if (f.casingRows.length > 0 || f.topRows.length > 0 || (expanded() && coordinateAxes().length > 0)) {
        <div class="wbp__facts">
          @if (f.casingRows.length > 0) {
            <section class="wbp__table wbp__table--casing" aria-label="Casing program">
              <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Casing program <span class="wbp__count">{{ f.casingRows.length }}</span></h3>
              <ul class="wbp__rows">
                @for (row of f.casingRows; track $index) {
                  <li class="wbp__row" [class.wbp__row--warn]="row.belowTarget">
                    <span class="wbp__row-size">{{ row.size }}</span>
                    <span class="wbp__row-type">{{ row.type }}</span>
                    <span class="wbp__row-depth">
                      {{ row.depth }}
                      @if (row.belowTarget) {
                        <span class="wbp__row-flag" title="Set deeper than the target depth">below TD</span>
                      }
                    </span>
                  </li>
                }
              </ul>
            </section>
          }
          @if (f.topRows.length > 0) {
            <section class="wbp__table wbp__table--tops" aria-label="Formation tops">
              <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Formation tops <span class="wbp__count">{{ f.topRows.length }}</span></h3>
              <ul class="wbp__rows wbp__rows--tops" [class.wbp__rows--interval]="isFilled()">
                @if (isFilled()) {
                  <li class="wbp__row wbp__row--head" aria-hidden="true">
                    <span>Top</span>
                    <span>Depth</span>
                    <span>Thick ft</span>
                  </li>
                }
                @for (row of f.topRows; track $index) {
                  <li class="wbp__row">
                    <span class="wbp__row-type wbp__row-type--code">{{ row.code }}</span>
                    <span class="wbp__row-depth">{{ row.depth }}</span>
                    @if (isFilled()) {
                      <span class="wbp__row-interval"><span class="cdk-visually-hidden">Thickness in feet </span>{{ row.interval }}</span>
                    }
                  </li>
                }
              </ul>
            </section>
          }
          <!-- Where the well is: Northing/Easting/UTM Zone split apart, shown exactly as the raw survey values (no
               lat/long conversion), each with the icon of its axis. Both maximised forms (the dialog and the Well
               Design tab's `fit` layout) — same gate as the metrics block and the formation tops' thickness column
               above. The compact editor-sidebar preview stays without it: that page already shows the same split in
               its own Request Reference rows, right next to this panel. -->
          @if (isFilled() && coordinateAxes().length > 0) {
            <section class="wbp__table wbp__table--location" aria-label="Location">
              <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow"><mat-icon class="wbp__eyebrow-icon" aria-hidden="true">location_on</mat-icon>Location</h3>
              <ul class="wbp__geo">
                <svg class="wbp__geo-globe" viewBox="0 0 64 64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                  <circle cx="32" cy="32" r="28" />
                  <ellipse cx="32" cy="32" rx="12" ry="28" />
                  <path d="M32 4v56M4 32h56M8.5 19h47M8.5 45h47" />
                </svg>
                @for (axis of coordinateAxes(); track axis.label) {
                  <li class="wbp__geo-axis">
                    <span class="wbp__geo-badge" aria-hidden="true"><mat-icon>{{ axis.icon }}</mat-icon></span>
                    <span class="wbp__geo-label">{{ axis.label }}</span>
                    <span class="wbp__geo-value">{{ axis.value }}</span>
                  </li>
                }
              </ul>
            </section>
          }
        </div>
      }
    </div>
  } @else {
    <div class="wbp__empty">
      <span class="wbp__empty-icon"><mat-icon>architecture</mat-icon></span>
      <p class="wbp__empty-title">Nothing to draw yet</p>
      <p class="wbp__empty-hint">{{ emptyHint() }}</p>
    </div>
  }
</section>

<!-- One reading: label, figure, and the well as a hairline with the figure's pictogram on it. -->
<ng-template #statTile let-stat let-i="i">
  <div class="wbp__stat" [class.wbp__stat--warn]="stat.warn" [style.--wbp-i]="i">
    <!-- The inline previews take the shorthand ("TD", "Static WL"): four figures beside a small drawing,
         read at a glance. The maximised forms have the room for the full label. -->
    <dt>
      {{ isFilled() ? stat.label : stat.short }}
      @if (stat.hint) {
        <span class="wbp__stat-hint">{{ stat.hint }}</span>
      }
    </dt>
    <dd>{{ stat.value }}</dd>
    @if (stat.gauge; as gauge) {
      <!-- How far down the well it is: the line is the well, surface to total depth, filled to the depth
           (or, for the water column, over the stretch it fills). No pictogram on the line — the fill
           itself (tinted per stat.glyph) is the marker. -->
      <span class="wbp__gauge wbp__gauge--{{ stat.glyph }}" aria-hidden="true">
        <i class="wbp__gauge-fill" [style.left.%]="gauge.fromPct" [style.width.%]="gauge.toPct - gauge.fromPct"></i>
      </span>
    }
  </div>
</ng-template>

<!-- Opened by Expand. The panel renders itself again, in its dialog form, so there is one
     implementation of the diagram, not two. -->
<ng-template #expandedTpl>
  <app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel
    [diagramData]="diagramData()"
    [hasData]="hasData()"
    [hasTargetDepth]="hasTargetDepth()"
    [layout]="expandedLayout"
    [title]="title()"
    [irCode]="irCode()"
    [coordinates]="coordinates()"
    [testType]="testType()"
    [expanded]="true"
    (closed)="collapse()"
  />
</ng-template>
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component.scss

```scss
@use '../../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;
@use '../../shared/wwell-material' as wmat;
@use '../wwell-ir-wellbore-theme' as wb;

// `ViewEncapsulation.None` (see the component) — everything is namespaced `.wbp`, and the
// `.wp-wellbore-dialog` block at the end styles the portalled Expand dialog's own surface.

app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel {
  display: block;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wbp,
.wbp *,
.wbp *::before,
.wbp *::after {
  // The Expand dialog is portalled outside any page that sets this.
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.wbp {
  // Depth gridlines: a quiet continuation of the axis ticks, never competing with the drawing.
  --wbp-grid: color-mix(in srgb, var(--wp-line-strong) 65%, transparent);
  // The sheen across a raised surface's upper corner. Transparent in the current theme (`--wp-glass-sheen`): cards are flat.
  --wbp-sheen: linear-gradient(160deg, var(--wp-glass-sheen), transparent 55%);
  // The panel's type scale. Nothing is set smaller than `micro` (12px, upper-case labels only); prose and chips
  // sit on `small`, table rows and facts on `body`, the figures on `figure`.
  --wbp-fs-micro: 0.75rem;
  --wbp-fs-small: 0.8125rem;
  --wbp-fs-body: 0.875rem;
  --wbp-fs-figure: 1.125rem;
  // The rhythm between the panel's blocks, and between a block's label and its card.
  --wbp-block-gap: 0.95rem;

  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: var(--wbp-block-gap);
  padding: 0.85rem 0.9rem 0.9rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-1;
  color: tokens.$navy;
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);
  @include wmat.buttons;
}

// ── Header ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.wbp__head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wbp__heading {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  min-width: 0;
}

// The well's name, in the mono face every other screen sets a well name in, and big: it is what the card is.
.wbp__title {
  position: relative;
  margin: 0;
  padding-left: 0.65rem;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-size: 1.375rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  letter-spacing: -0.012em;
  color: tokens.$navy;

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 0.12em;
    bottom: 0.12em;
    width: 3px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: tokens.$accent;
  }
}

.wbp__sub {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.15rem 0.5rem;
  margin: 0.2rem 0 0 0.65rem;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

// The record's IR number under its well's name in the dialog: the accent, in the mono face.
.wbp__sub-code {
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
  color: tokens.$accent;
}

// The IR's test type in the dialog's header, as the one word it is with its icon, and nothing round it: PUMP in the accent,
// FLOW in the drawing's water colour (both follow the theme). Set in the mono face the panel's figures use.
.wbp__mode {
  // The pump pictogram cuts its detail lines out in `--wp-surface-alt`; here that is the dialog's own surface.
  --wp-surface-alt: var(--wp-surface-raised);
  --wbp-mode: var(--wp-accent);

  flex: none;
  align-self: center;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem 0 auto;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-size: 1.0625rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--wbp-mode);
  cursor: default;

  mat-icon {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    font-size: 22px;
  }

  // Beats the glyph's own `:host([data-kind='pump'])` size.
  app-wwell-ir-wellbore-glyph[data-kind='pump'] {
    width: 24px;
    height: 21px;
  }
}

.wbp__mode--flow {
  --wbp-mode: var(--wp-wb-water);
}

.wbp__action {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  padding: 0;
  color: tokens.$muted;

  &:hover {
    color: tokens.$accent;
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    font-size: 18px;
  }
}

// ── Key depths ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Brief form (the inline previews, which is where a new IR is filled in): the four figures share one card as tiles —
// shorthand label over the figure, as many across as fit — with no gauges. The drawing beside them already shows
// where each one sits on the well, so the card is a read-out, not a second picture of it.
.wbp:not(.wbp--expanded) {
  // Two across, always: four figures make two even rows, where `auto-fit` would leave one alone on a ragged third.
  .wbp__stats {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .wbp__stat {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    row-gap: 0;
    padding: 0.35rem 0.7rem 0.4rem;
  }

  .wbp__stat dd {
    text-align: left;
  }

  .wbp__gauge {
    display: none;
  }
}

// A list, not a pile of tiles: every row has the same three parts on the same lines — label on the left, the
// figure right-aligned in one column, and under them the gauge (the well as a hairline, surface to total depth, at
// one length for every row so the rows compare). Rows are cut from one card by hairlines.
.wbp__block {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wbp__stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 1px;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--wp-line-soft);
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-soft);
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__stat {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  align-items: baseline;
  column-gap: 0.75rem;
  row-gap: 0.1rem;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: var(--wbp-stat-pad, 0.5rem 0.8rem 0.4rem);
  background: var(--wbp-sheen), tokens.$surface;
  animation: wbp-rise tokens.$duration-slow tokens.$ease-out backwards;
  animation-delay: calc(var(--wbp-i, 0) * 45ms);

  dt {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: 0 0.5rem;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: tokens.$subtle;
  }

  dd {
    margin: 0;
    font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-figure);
    font-weight: 600;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    text-align: right;
    white-space: nowrap;
    color: tokens.$navy-deep;
  }
}

// The gauge fills the row's foot from the surface down ("how much below ground") with its pictogram on the
// end; the water column fills the stretch it occupies.
.wbp__gauge {
  --wbp-tone: color-mix(in srgb, var(--wp-wb-outline) 60%, transparent);

  position: relative;
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  height: var(--wbp-gauge-h, 16px);

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 50% 0 auto;
    height: 3px;
    margin-top: -1.5px;
    border-radius: 2px;
    background: var(--wp-line);
  }
}

.wbp__gauge--water {
  --wbp-tone: var(--wp-wb-water);
}

.wbp__gauge--pump {
  --wbp-tone: var(--wp-accent);
}

.wbp__gauge-fill {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  display: block;
  height: 3px;
  margin-top: -1.5px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: var(--wbp-tone);
}

// The qualifier beside the label ("9″ Liner"): prose, not another upper-case label.
.wbp__stat-hint {
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: 0;
  text-transform: none;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

.wbp .wbp__stat--warn {
  background-image: var(--wbp-sheen), linear-gradient(var(--wp-warning-soft), var(--wp-warning-soft));

  dd {
    color: var(--wp-warning);
  }

  .wbp__stat-hint {
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--wp-warning);
  }
}

// Section label shared across the panel's cards.
.wbp__eyebrow {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.4rem;
  // As tall as the count badge some carry, so a label and its card start on the same line in every column.
  min-height: 1.15rem;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: tokens.$subtle;
}

.wbp__count {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  min-width: 1.25rem;
  height: 1.15rem;
  padding: 0 0.35rem;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-pill;
  background: tokens.$surface-sunken;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  letter-spacing: 0;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

// ── Stage: the depth-gridded drawing surface ───────────────────────
.wbp__stage {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  container-type: inline-size;
  padding: 0.6rem 0.5rem 0.5rem 0;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-soft;
}

// Width is the smallest of: the stage, the max width, and the width at which the max height is
// reached — so the aspect ratio always holds and the SVGs (`preserveAspectRatio="none"`) never
// stretch.
.wbp__figure {
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  flex: none;
  width: min(100cqw, var(--wbp-max-width, 100cqw), calc(var(--wbp-max-height, 9999px) * var(--wbp-ratio)));
  aspect-ratio: var(--wbp-ratio);
}

.wbp__plot {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1 1 0;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wbp__grid {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  pointer-events: none;

  line {
    stroke: var(--wbp-grid);
    stroke-width: 1;
    stroke-dasharray: 2 5;
    vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke;
  }
}

// The ground line. The renderer draws its own between two fixed offsets that are only centred on the bore in the one
// coordinate space they were tuned for, so in every other form it sat lopsided on the well. That one is hidden and the
// panel draws one in the grid layer instead (`groundLine` in the component), centred on the bore.
// The diagram's SVG is stretched to fit its box, so a hairline lands at a fractional thickness and y and is anti-aliased
// across two pixel rows — blurred, and different on every surface. Snapped to the pixel grid and squared off (round caps
// distort under the stretch), it is one even line everywhere.
.wbp__stage .ground-line {
  display: none;
}

.wbp__grid .wbp__ground-line {
  stroke: var(--wp-wb-ground);
  stroke-width: 3;
  stroke-dasharray: none;
  stroke-linecap: butt;
  vector-effect: none;
  shape-rendering: crispEdges;
}

// The renderer sets the "TOTAL DEPTH" label's baseline a fixed 35 units under the deepest element, so its top edge rises
// as the type grows (20 → 31 units across this panel's forms) until it meets the completion's own bottom labels
// ("Screen", "Gravel Pack", which hang ~6 units below it). Shifting it down by the cap height (~0.72em) puts its top
// edge the same $td-label-gap under the drawing in every form; `em` follows whatever size the rules below give it.
// The layouts leave room for it under the drawing (wwell-ir-preview-diagram-layout.ts).
$td-label-renderer-baseline: 35;
$td-label-gap: 30;

.wbp__stage .total-depth-main {
  transform: translateY(calc(0.72em - #{$td-label-renderer-baseline - $td-label-gap}px));
}

.wbp__diagram {
  position: relative;
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
}

// Hides the D3-rendered "TOTAL DEPTH: 0 FT" label until a target depth actually exists — and, since
// the scale then ends at a placeholder depth, stops calling its last tick out as the target.
.wbp__figure--no-depth .td-label {
  display: none;
}

.wbp .wbp__figure--no-depth {
  .tick-line--target {
    stroke: tokens.$muted;
  }

  .tick-label--target {
    fill: tokens.$navy-deep;
    font-weight: 600;
  }
}

// Beats the diagram's own `[_nghost] .geo-axis`-style rules (0,2,0): this compiles to (0,3,0).
.wbp .wbp__stage {
  @include wb.wellbore-theme($ng-deep: false);
}

// The figures behind the drawing: stacked in the sidebar, and where there is room (the view page, Sections view) the
// three cards — key depths, casing program, formation tops — stand side by side, each the width the sidebar gives it.
.wbp__data {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: var(--wbp-block-gap) 1rem;
}

.wbp:not(.wbp--compact):not(.wbp--expanded) {
  .wbp__data {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(17rem, 1fr));
    align-items: start;
  }

  .wbp__facts {
    display: contents;
  }
}

// The drawing's own type is set in viewBox units, so what it prints at follows the drawing's size: at the sidebar's ~200px
// the depth numerals, geologic labels and total-depth label came to ~11px against leader-less titles at 7–8 — heavier
// than the well they annotate. Brought down to about 9.5px (24 units; the ground label and casing sizes a little
// under), and the axis and its ticks set lighter, so the bore and the casing read first and the scale recedes. The wider
// forms keep their 12px+ type. WWELL_IR_SIDEBAR_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT's depth axis has room to spare at this size.
.wbp--compact .wbp__stage {
  .tick-label,
  .geo-depth,
  .geo-code {
    font-size: 24px;
  }

  .tick-label:not(.tick-label--target) {
    font-weight: 500;
  }

  .axis-line,
  .tick-line {
    stroke-width: 1.5;
    opacity: 0.7;
  }

  .geo-axis,
  .geo-tick:not(.geo-tick--target) {
    opacity: 0.7;
  }

  // The two-line titles keep the renderer's fixed line spacing (19–20 units), which caps their type.
  .axis-title {
    font-size: 18px;
  }

  .column-label {
    font-size: 19px;
  }

  .ground-label {
    font-size: 21px;
  }

  // Centred on the bore, so its width is what keeps it inside the drawing: 20 characters at 24 units leave room.
  .total-depth-main {
    font-size: 24px;
  }

  .casing-size-inner {
    font-size: 23px;
  }
}

// The card's own type steps down with the drawing: the well's name a size smaller, the four figures one step under the
// maximised views' (the labels stay on the 12px floor).
.wbp--compact {
  .wbp__title {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
  }

  .wbp__sub {
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  }

  .wbp__stat dd {
    font-size: 0.9375rem;
  }
}

// Sidebar form: the card fills the sidebar exactly — the well name, the drawing in whatever height is left, and the
// four key depths under it — so nothing is cut off at the foot and nothing has to be scrolled to. The legend and the
// casing / formation-top tables are left out here: the editor prints both, in its own Horizons and Casing sections a
// few inches to the left, and the drawing's symbols are named in the maximised view where they can be read.
// The host is as tall as the column the sidebar gives it, and the card fills it.
.wbp-host--compact {
  height: 100%;
}

.wbp--compact {
  height: 100%;

  .wbp__stage {
    flex: 1;
    // Below this the drawing's own numerals fall under 9px; a window too short for it scrolls the card instead.
    min-height: 22rem;
    container-type: size;
  }

  .wbp__figure {
    width: min(100cqw, calc(100cqh * var(--wbp-ratio)));
  }

  .wbp__block--legend,
  .wbp__facts {
    display: none;
  }
}

// Sidebar form: the leader labels render at ~6px and collide there, so they are left to Expand (showing them would
// cost ~145px of drawing each — a ~500px sidebar). Hover tooltips on the casing and the depth/geologic labels still
// work. The water arrow goes with the water label it points at, so nothing in the drawing points at a label that is
// not there.
.wbp--compact .wbp__stage {
  .casing-label__primary,
  .casing-label__secondary,
  .casing-label__line,
  .casing-label__arrow,
  .oh-label-line,
  .oh-label-arrow,
  .open-hole-text,
  .screen-label-line,
  .screen-label-text,
  .pump-leader-group,
  .water-label,
  .water-arrow {
    display: none;
  }
}

// ── Legend ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// A grid, so the swatches stand in one column and the names start on one line; the columns are as many as fit.
.wbp__legend {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(7.5rem, 1fr));
  gap: 0.55rem 0.75rem;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0.7rem 0.8rem;
  list-style: none;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-soft);
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__key {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.4rem minmax(0, 1fr);
  align-items: center;
  column-gap: 0.5rem;
  overflow: hidden;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 1.2;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

.wbp__swatch {
  justify-self: center;
  display: inline-block;
  width: 14px;
  height: 12px;
}

// Swatches mirror how each element is drawn, so the legend reads as a key, not decoration.
.wbp__swatch--ground,
.wbp__swatch--water,
.wbp__swatch--tops {
  height: 0;
  align-self: center;
}

.wbp__swatch--ground {
  border-top: 2px solid var(--wp-wb-ground);
}

.wbp__swatch--water {
  border-top: 2px dashed var(--wp-wb-water);
}

.wbp__swatch--tops {
  width: 10px;
  border-top: 2px solid var(--wp-wb-outline);
}

.wbp__swatch--casing,
.wbp__swatch--liner {
  border-radius: 1px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #7c8798 0%, #dfe5ee 48%, #7c8798 100%);
}

.wbp__swatch--casing {
  width: 10px;
}

.wbp__swatch--liner {
  width: 7px;
}

.wbp__swatch--open-hole {
  width: 10px;
  border: 1.5px dashed var(--wp-wb-outline);
  border-top: 0;
}

.wbp__swatch--gravel {
  background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--wp-subtle) 1px, transparent 1.6px) 0 0 / 4px 4px;
}

.wbp__swatch--screen {
  background:
    repeating-linear-gradient(0deg, transparent 0 2px, var(--wp-subtle) 2px 3px),
    repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 2px, var(--wp-subtle) 2px 3px);
  opacity: 0.8;
}

.wbp__swatch--perforated {
  width: 10px;
  border-right: 2px dotted var(--wp-wb-outline);
  border-left: 2px dotted var(--wp-wb-outline);
}

// ── Casing program + formation tops ────────────────────────────────
// These also stand in for the leader labels the compact sidebar hides.
.wbp__facts {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(15rem, 1fr));
  gap: var(--wbp-block-gap) 1rem;
}

.wbp__table {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  min-width: 0;
}

// A 1px grid gap over a tinted backing draws the hairline row dividers with no per-row borders. The list owns the
// columns and each row takes them from it (subgrid), so the sizes, types and depths line up down the card whatever a
// row holds; where subgrid is missing a row sizes its own columns, as it always did.
.wbp__rows {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: max-content minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  gap: 1px;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  list-style: none;
  background: var(--wp-line-soft);
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-soft);
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__rows--tops {
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
}

.wbp__row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: subgrid;
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  align-items: baseline;
  column-gap: 0.75rem;
  padding: var(--wbp-row-pad, 0.45rem 0.8rem);
  background-color: tokens.$surface;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-body);
}

.wbp__row-size,
.wbp__row-depth {
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wbp__row-type {
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

.wbp__row-type--code {
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy;
}

.wbp__row-depth {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 0.4rem;
}

.wbp .wbp__row--warn {
  background-image: linear-gradient(var(--wp-warning-soft), var(--wp-warning-soft));

  .wbp__row-depth {
    color: var(--wp-warning);
  }
}

.wbp__row-flag {
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--wp-warning);
}

// ── Maximised extras: thickness column, well metrics ──
// The formation tops gain a third column — the interval down to the next top — under a header row of its own.
.wbp__rows--interval {
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto auto;

  .wbp__row {
    column-gap: 0.55rem;
  }
}

.wbp__row--head {
  padding-block: 0.2rem;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: tokens.$subtle;
  background-color: tokens.$surface-alt;

  span:not(:first-child) {
    text-align: right;
  }
}

.wbp__row-interval {
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-weight: 500;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  text-align: right;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

// ── Location card (Expand dialog) ──────────────────────────────────
// The one card in the rails that is lit, and it stands at the foot of the right rail: an accent wash over the surface, an
// accent rule down its edge, a faint graticule globe in its corner, and per axis a solid accent badge (up-down for
// latitude, left-right for longitude) beside the degrees as written, the hemisphere as a chip.
.wbp__eyebrow-icon {
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
  font-size: 16px;
  color: tokens.$accent;
}

.wbp__geo {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  list-style: none;
  background:
    linear-gradient(150deg, var(--wp-accent-soft), transparent 75%),
    tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--wp-accent) 30%, var(--wp-line-soft));
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0 auto 0 0;
    width: 3px;
    background: tokens.$accent;
  }
}

// A wireframe globe, drawn in the accent, rising from the card's right edge and kept clear of the figures: it says "place"
// without carrying any data.
.wbp__geo-globe {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  right: -2.6rem;
  width: 6.5rem;
  height: 6.5rem;
  fill: none;
  stroke: var(--wp-accent);
  stroke-width: 1.1;
  opacity: 0.16;
  pointer-events: none;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
}

.wbp__geo-axis {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0, 1fr);
  grid-template-areas:
    'badge label'
    'badge value';
  align-items: center;
  column-gap: 0.7rem;
  padding: 0.55rem 0.8rem 0.55rem 0.95rem;
  animation: wbp-rise tokens.$duration-slow tokens.$ease-out backwards;

  & + & {
    border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--wp-accent) 18%, var(--wp-line-soft));
    animation-delay: 60ms;
  }
}

.wbp__geo-label {
  grid-area: label;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: tokens.$subtle;
}

.wbp__geo-badge {
  grid-area: badge;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: 11px;
  background: var(--wp-accent-fill);
  color: #ffffff;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px color-mix(in srgb, var(--wp-accent-fill) 35%, transparent);

  mat-icon {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    font-size: 22px;
  }
}

.wbp__geo-value {
  grid-area: value;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  // A raw UTM northing/easting ("3043496.7774") runs longer than the old decimal-degree value
  // this card used to show — shown in full (no rounding), so it wraps instead of overflowing the
  // rail's fixed width, rather than a `nowrap` forcing it past the card's edge.
  font-size: 1.0625rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  line-height: 1.25;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  overflow-wrap: break-word;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

// ── Empty ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.wbp__empty {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.3rem;
  padding: 1.5rem 1rem 1.3rem;
  text-align: center;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__empty-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
  background: var(--wp-accent-soft);
  color: tokens.$accent;

  mat-icon {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    font-size: 22px;
  }
}

.wbp__empty-title {
  margin: 0.35rem 0 0;
  font-size: 0.86rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy;
}

.wbp__empty-hint {
  margin: 0;
  max-width: 32ch;
  font-size: 0.74rem;
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

// ── Motion ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Entrance only, once, on compositor properties: the bar draws in from the surface, its markers drop
// on, the tiles rise in turn.
@keyframes wbp-rise {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(6px);
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .wbp,
  .wbp *,
  .wbp *::before,
  .wbp *::after {
    animation: none !important;
    transition: none !important;
  }
}

// ── Expanded (dialog) form ─────────────────────────────────────────
// The maximised view: nothing in it scrolls. The dialog takes most of the window (see `expand()`); inside it the
// drawing takes the left with its key in one line right under it, and the figures stand in a rail beside them. The
// figure is sized off the stage's own box (container query units) so it fills its area at the diagram's true aspect
// ratio instead of being cropped or stretched. The rail is set tight enough to fit a 900px-high window whole; from
// 980px up (`wbp-dialog`) it gets the room back.
.wbp--expanded {
  --wbp-block-gap: 0.6rem;
  --wbp-stat-pad: 0.26rem 0.8rem 0.2rem;
  --wbp-gauge-h: 12px;
  --wbp-row-pad: 0.22rem 0.8rem;

  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) clamp(19.5rem, 22vw, 23rem);
  grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  grid-template-areas:
    'head   head'
    'stage  data'
    'legend data';
  gap: 0.65rem 1.25rem;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 0.85rem 1.25rem 0.75rem;
  background: transparent;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 0;
  box-shadow: none;

  .wbp__head {
    grid-area: head;
  }

  // The rail: key depths, then the two tables in what is left.
  .wbp__data {
    grid-area: data;
    grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr);
    min-height: 0;
  }

  .wbp__stage {
    grid-area: stage;
    align-items: center;
    min-height: 0;
    container-type: size;
    padding: 0.75rem 1rem 0.75rem 0;
  }

  // The key: one line under the drawing, in the drawing's own column. No label of its own (the section is named for
  // assistive tech); the entries sit close together and never wrap.
  .wbp__block--legend {
    grid-area: legend;
  }

  .wbp__block--legend .wbp__eyebrow {
    display: none;
  }

  .wbp__legend {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 0 1.1rem;
    padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem;
  }

  .wbp__key {
    display: flex;
    flex: 0 0 auto;
    gap: 0.45rem;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-body);
  }

  // Only if the window is too short for the rail after all does it scroll, on its own. A column, so the location card
  // can stand at its foot (`margin-top: auto`) while the tables keep their place at the top.
  .wbp__facts {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-height: 0;
    overflow-y: auto;

    > * {
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
  }

  .wbp__table--location {
    margin-top: auto;
  }

  .wbp__title {
    font-size: 1.5rem;
  }

  .wbp__sub {
    font-size: 0.875rem;
  }

  .wbp__stat dd {
    font-size: 1.0625rem;
  }

  .wbp__figure {
    width: min(100cqw, calc(100cqh * var(--wbp-ratio)));
  }
}

// A taller window gives the rail its room back.
@container wbp-dialog (min-height: 980px) {
  .wbp--expanded {
    .wbp__block--stats {
      --wbp-stat-pad: 0.5rem 0.8rem 0.4rem;
      --wbp-gauge-h: 16px;
    }

    .wbp__facts {
      --wbp-row-pad: 0.45rem 0.8rem;
      --wbp-block-gap: 0.95rem;
    }

    .wbp__stat dd {
      font-size: 1.125rem;
    }
  }
}

// The drawing's own type, in viewBox units, so what it prints at follows the stage's size. Two steps, each taken when
// the stage is small enough in *either* direction (a tall, narrow stage draws the well just as small as a short one):
// the first lifts everything about a third, the second again for a stage at about half scale. Two caps hold through
// both: the two-line titles ("Geologic Horizons", "DEPTH (FT BGL)") stay ≤19 units because the renderer's line
// spacing is fixed at 19–20, and the casing remarks stay at 21 or they run past the drawing's right edge.
// `@container` here asks the stage itself — the nearest container above each label.
@mixin stage-type-step-1 {
  .tick-label {
    font-size: 24px;
  }

  .geo-depth,
  .geo-code {
    font-size: 22px;
  }

  .axis-title {
    font-size: 18px;
  }

  .column-label {
    font-size: 19px;
  }

  .ground-label {
    font-size: 23px;
  }

  .total-depth-main {
    font-size: 27px;
  }

  .casing-label__primary,
  .water-label,
  .pump-label {
    font-size: 26px;
  }

  .casing-label__secondary {
    font-size: 21px;
  }

  .open-hole-text,
  .screen-label-text {
    font-size: 25px;
  }

  .casing-size-inner {
    font-size: 28px;
  }
}

@mixin stage-type-step-2 {
  .tick-label {
    font-size: 28px;
  }

  .geo-depth,
  .geo-code {
    font-size: 24px;
  }

  .ground-label {
    font-size: 27px;
  }

  .total-depth-main {
    font-size: 31px;
  }

  .casing-label__primary,
  .water-label,
  .pump-label {
    font-size: 30px;
  }

  .open-hole-text,
  .screen-label-text {
    font-size: 29px;
  }

  .casing-size-inner {
    font-size: 30px;
  }
}

@container (max-height: 680px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-1;
  }
}

@container (max-width: 950px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-1;
  }
}

@container (max-height: 600px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-2;
  }
}

@container (max-width: 780px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-2;
  }
}

// A big dialog draws the well at a large scale, so its type can grow with it: everything a step up from the base sizes.
// The ranges sit outside both small-stage steps above, so only a roomy stage ever takes this.
@container (min-width: 1100px) and (min-height: 720px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    .tick-label {
      font-size: 22px;
    }

    .geo-depth,
    .geo-code {
      font-size: 23px;
    }

    .casing-label__primary,
    .water-label,
    .pump-label {
      font-size: 23px;
    }

    .casing-label__secondary {
      font-size: 19px;
    }

    .open-hole-text,
    .screen-label-text {
      font-size: 22px;
    }

    .total-depth-main {
      font-size: 24px;
    }

    .ground-label {
      font-size: 21px;
    }
  }
}

// Under ~900px of stage the browser sets small text a little wider than its box scales to, so the leader labels take
// one unit off here — last, so it holds whichever step above applied — and the widest of them ("18" Intermediate
// @ 1,500") still ends inside the drawing.
@container (max-width: 900px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    .casing-label__primary,
    .water-label,
    .pump-label {
      font-size: 29px;
    }

    .casing-label__secondary {
      font-size: 20px;
    }
  }
}

// Not enough width for a rail: stack everything and let the dialog body scroll. The stage needs a
// definite height of its own then (it is a size container), so it gets one.
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .wbp--expanded {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    overflow-y: auto;

    .wbp__stage {
      flex: none;
      height: min(70vh, 560px);
    }

    .wbp__data {
      flex: none;
      grid-template-rows: none;
    }

    .wbp__facts {
      overflow-y: visible;
    }

    .wbp__legend {
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      justify-content: flex-start;
    }
  }
}

// The panel's host is the container the maximised view asks about its size (`@container wbp-dialog`): it is as tall
// and wide as the dialog, and `.wbp--expanded` re-grids itself by it.
.wp-wellbore-dialog app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel,
.wbp-host--fit {
  container: wbp-dialog / size;
}

// ── Fit form (the view page's Well Design step) ────────────────────
// The maximised layout, inline: the host is as tall as the page it sits in (its parent gives it a height), and the
// panel is a card again. The drawing and its key stand left, the figures right; the page already names the well and
// the step, so the title is kept for assistive tech only and Expand floats over the drawing's own row. The rail is
// set as tight as a laptop-height page needs, and is given its room back as it grows.
.wbp-host--fit {
  height: 100%;
}

.wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
  --wbp-block-gap: 0.5rem;
  --wbp-stat-pad: 0.24rem 0.7rem 0.18rem;
  --wbp-gauge-h: 8px;
  --wbp-row-pad: 0.2rem 0.7rem;

  position: relative;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 14.5rem;
  grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  grid-template-areas:
    'stage  data'
    'legend legend';
  gap: 0.4rem 0.7rem;
  padding: 0.45rem 0.6rem 0.5rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-1;

  .wbp__head {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0.62rem;
    right: 0.85rem;
    z-index: 2;
    grid-area: auto;
  }

  .wbp__heading {
    position: absolute;
    width: 1px;
    height: 1px;
    overflow: hidden;
    clip-path: inset(50%);
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .wbp__action {
    width: 26px;
    height: 26px;
  }

  .wbp__stage {
    padding: 0.35rem 0.4rem 0.35rem 0;
  }

  .wbp__legend {
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    justify-content: space-evenly;
    gap: 0 0.6rem;
    padding: 0.4rem 0.6rem;
  }

  .wbp__title {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
  }
}

// The rail's density follows the page height: below ~780px the gauges shrink to a hairline, below ~700 they go.
@container wbp-dialog (max-height: 780px) {
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    --wbp-stat-pad: 0.2rem 0.7rem 0.16rem;
    --wbp-gauge-h: 3px;
    --wbp-row-pad: 0.14rem 0.7rem;
    --wbp-block-gap: 0.4rem;
  }
}

@container wbp-dialog (max-height: 700px) {
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    --wbp-stat-pad: 0.14rem 0.7rem 0.12rem;
    --wbp-row-pad: 0.1rem 0.7rem;
    --wbp-block-gap: 0.3rem;

    .wbp__gauge,
    .wbp__stat-hint {
      display: none;
    }

    .wbp__row,
    .wbp__stat {
      line-height: 1.15;
    }

    // Still on the scale (12px), one step down from the rows' usual size.
    .wbp__row,
    .wbp__stat dt {
      font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
    }

    .wbp__stat dd {
      font-size: 0.9375rem;
    }

    .wbp__eyebrow {
      min-height: 0.9rem;
    }

    .wbp__legend {
      padding: 0.28rem 0.6rem;
    }
  }
}

.wp-wellbore-dialog {
  .mat-mdc-dialog-surface {
    background: tokens.$surface-raised !important;
    border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
    border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg !important;
    box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-3 !important;
    color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel {
    height: 100%;
  }
}

// ── Both sides of the well (a wide maximised view) ─────────────────
// Wide enough to read the drawing from both sides: the well stands in the middle, the depth figures it is measured by
// (key depths) on the left — the side its ruler is drawn on — and the two programmes the leader labels on its right
// name (casing, then formation tops) on the right. The key stays under the drawing, as its footnote. Each side is
// shorter than one stacked rail, so a laptop-height window fits without scrolling too. Last in the file on purpose:
// it also re-grids the inline `fit` form, whose own rule has the same weight.
@container wbp-dialog (min-width: 1140px) {
  .wbp--expanded,
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    // The sides grow with the dialog (`cqw` is its width), so the well keeps the middle at any size.
    grid-template-columns: clamp(14rem, 16cqw, 20rem) minmax(0, 1fr) clamp(14rem, 16cqw, 20rem);
    grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    grid-template-areas:
      'head  head  head'
      'stats stage facts'
      '.     legend .';

    .wbp__data {
      display: contents;
    }

    .wbp__block--stats {
      grid-area: stats;
      align-content: start;
      min-height: 0;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }

    .wbp__facts {
      grid-area: facts;
    }

    // In a column this narrow a label and its figure cannot share a line ("STATIC WATER LEVEL" alone is most of it),
    // so each key depth becomes a tile: label over figure, the gauge under both. It reads as a column of readings
    // beside the well, and fills the height the two tables take on the other side.
    .wbp__stat {
      grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
      row-gap: 0.05rem;
      padding: 0.42rem 0.8rem 0.38rem;

      dd {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        text-align: left;
      }
    }

    .wbp__stat-hint {
      width: 100%;
    }

    // Nothing in the left rail may shrink to fit — a rail too short for what it holds scrolls, it does not clip.
    .wbp__block--stats > * {
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
  }

  // The inline form has no header row of its own (its one action floats over the drawing).
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    grid-template-areas:
      'stats stage facts'
      '.     legend .';
  }
}

// The key sizes itself by the room its own row has, not by the dialog's: under the drawing it has the middle column,
// in the stacked forms the whole width. Measured, its eight entries need about 857px at `body` with roomy gaps,
// 749 at `small`, 696 at `micro` — so each step down is taken just before they would wrap.
.wbp--expanded .wbp__block--legend {
  container: wbp-key / inline-size;
}

@container wbp-key (max-width: 875px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__legend {
    gap: 0 0.625rem;
  }

  .wbp--expanded .wbp__key {
    gap: 0.4rem;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
  }
}

@container wbp-key (max-width: 765px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__legend {
    gap: 0 0.5rem;
  }

  .wbp--expanded .wbp__key {
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  }
}

@container wbp-key (max-width: 710px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__legend {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: flex-start;
  }
}
```

import {
